  }

  function extractBalancedJsonObject(value) {
    const text=String(value||'');const start=text.indexOf('{');if(start<0)return '';
    let depth=0,inStraight=false,inCurly=false,escape=false;
    for(let i=start;i<text.length;i++){
      const ch=text[i];
      if(escape){escape=false;continue;}
      if(inStraight&&ch==='\\'){escape=true;continue;}
      if(!inCurly&&ch==='"'){inStraight=!inStraight;continue;}
      if(!inStraight&&ch==='“'&&!inCurly){inCurly=true;continue;}
      if(!inStraight&&ch==='”'&&inCurly){inCurly=false;continue;}
      if(inStraight||inCurly)continue;
      if(ch==='{')depth++;
      else if(ch==='}'&&--depth===0)return text.slice(start,i+1);
    }
    return '';
  }

  function manualLoreJsonCandidates(raw) {
    const original=String(raw||'').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'').trim(),candidates=[];
    const push=value=>{const text=String(value||'').trim();if(text&&!candidates.includes(text))candidates.push(text);};
    push(original);
    const fence=original.match(/^```(?:json|javascript|js)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```$/i);if(fence)push(fence[1]);
    const marker=original.match(/\[\[(WISH_LORE_V[12])\]\]/i);
    if(marker){
      const after=original.slice(marker.index+marker[0].length),close=new RegExp(`\\[\\[\\/${marker[1]}\\]\\]`,'i'),closing=after.match(close);
      push(closing?after.slice(0,closing.index):after);
    }
    for(const value of [...candidates]){const balanced=extractBalancedJsonObject(value);if(balanced)push(balanced);}
    return {original,candidates,markerVersion:marker?marker[1].toUpperCase():''};
  }

  function parseManualLoreResult(raw) {
    const prepared=manualLoreJsonCandidates(raw);let data=null,lastError=null;
    for(const candidate of prepared.candidates){
      for(const variant of [candidate,normalizeSmartJsonQuotes(candidate)]){
        try{data=JSON.parse(variant);break;}catch(error){lastError=error;}
      }
      if(data)break;
    }
    if(!data)throw new Error(`자료집 결과 JSON을 읽을 수 없습니다. 스마트 따옴표·코드블록·닫는 마커 누락은 자동 보정했지만 JSON 본문이 유효하지 않습니다.${lastError?.message?` (${lastError.message})`:''}`);
    const matchVersion=prepared.markerVersion;
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data) || !['WISH_LORE_V1','WISH_LORE_V2'].includes(data.version) || (matchVersion && matchVersion !== data.version) || (!matchVersion && data.version === 'WISH_LORE_V1') || !Array.isArray(data.operations) || Object.keys(data).some(key => !['version','operations'].includes(key))) throw new Error('자료집 결과는 올바른 version과 operations만 포함하는 증분 JSON이어야 합니다.');
    if (data.operations.length > 200) throw new Error('자료집 결과 작업은 한 번에 200개를 넘을 수 없습니다.');
    const seen = new Set(), declaredNewPacks = new Set();
    for (const [index, op] of data.operations.entries()) {
      if (!op || typeof op !== 'object' || Array.isArray(op) || !['pack_upsert','card_upsert'].includes(op.op)) throw new Error(`${index + 1}번째 작업의 op가 올바르지 않습니다. 삭제 작업은 지원하지 않습니다.`);
      const packOp = op.op === 'pack_upsert';
      const allowed = packOp ? ['op','ref','title','kind','sourceTurnKey','evidence'] : data.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2' ? ['op','ref','packRef','title','type','aliases','triggers','entities','full','compact','micro','exactQuote','speaker','target','context','location','date','sourceTurnKey','evidence'] : ['op','ref','packRef','title','type','aliases','triggers','summary','content','sourceTurnKey','evidence'];
      const unexpected = Object.keys(op).find(key => !allowed.includes(key));
      if (unexpected) throw new Error(`${index + 1}번째 작업에 허용되지 않은 ${unexpected} 필드가 있습니다. 주입 설정과 영구 ID는 AI 결과에서 변경할 수 없습니다.`);
      const refPattern = packOp ? /^(?:P\d{3,}|NEW_P[1-9]\d*)$/ : /^(?:C\d{3,}|NEW_C[1-9]\d*)$/;
      if (typeof op.ref !== 'string' || !refPattern.test(op.ref) || seen.has(op.ref)) throw new Error(`${index + 1}번째 작업의 REF가 잘못됐거나 중복됐습니다.`);
      seen.add(op.ref);
      if (typeof op.title !== 'string' || !op.title.trim() || op.title.length > 160) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 제목이 없거나 너무 깁니다.`);
      if (op.sourceTurnKey !== undefined && (typeof op.sourceTurnKey !== 'string' || !op.sourceTurnKey.trim())) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: sourceTurnKey가 올바르지 않습니다.`);
      if (op.evidence !== undefined && (typeof op.evidence !== 'string' || !op.evidence.trim())) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: evidence가 올바르지 않습니다.`);
      if (packOp) {
        if (!['room','reference'].includes(op.kind)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 자료팩 kind가 올바르지 않습니다.`);
      } else {
        if (typeof op.packRef !== 'string' || !/^(?:P\d{3,}|NEW_P[1-9]\d*)$/.test(op.packRef)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 대상 자료팩 REF가 올바르지 않습니다.`);
        if (!(data.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2' ? LORE_TYPES : LORE_TYPES.filter(type => !['복장','핵심 대사'].includes(type))).includes(op.type)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 허용되지 않은 자료 유형입니다.`);
        if (data.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2' && op.packRef.startsWith('NEW_') && !declaredNewPacks.has(op.packRef)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 신규 자료팩은 카드보다 먼저 선언해야 합니다.`);
        if (data.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2') {
          for (const field of ['full','compact','micro']) if (typeof op[field] !== 'string' || !op[field].trim()) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: ${field}은 비어 있거나 문자열이 아닙니다.`);
          for (const field of ['exactQuote','speaker','target','context','location','date']) if (typeof op[field] !== 'string') throw new Error(`${op.ref}: ${field}은 문자열이어야 합니다. 모르면 빈 문자열을 사용하세요.`);
          if (op.type === '핵심 대사' && (!String(op.exactQuote || '').trim() || !String(op.speaker || '').trim() || !String(op.context || '').trim())) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 핵심 대사는 원문 대사·화자·맥락이 필요합니다.`);
        } else if (typeof op.summary !== 'string' || typeof op.content !== 'string' || (!op.summary.trim() && !op.content.trim())) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 요약과 상세 내용이 모두 비어 있습니다.`);
        for (const field of data.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2' ? ['aliases','triggers','entities'] : ['aliases','triggers']) {
          if (!Array.isArray(op[field]) || op[field].length > 50 || op[field].some(value => typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim() || value.length > 120)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: ${field} 배열이 올바르지 않습니다.`);
        }
      }
      if (packOp && op.ref.startsWith('NEW_')) declaredNewPacks.add(op.ref);
    }
    return data;
  }

  function assertManualLoreEvidence(op, draft) {
    const key = String(op.sourceTurnKey || '');
    const evidence = String(op.evidence || '').trim();
    if (!key || evidence.length < 2 || evidence.length > 300) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 신규·실질 수정에는 sourceTurnKey와 2~300자의 evidence가 필요합니다.`);
    if(draft.recovery&&draft.sourceVerified===false&&draft.explicitUnknownApproval)return;
    const turn = draft.sourceTurns.find(row => row[0] === key);
    if (!turn) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: sourceTurnKey가 이번 작업 패킷의 RP 턴 식별값이 아닙니다.`);
    const normalize = value => String(value).replace(/\[(?:USER|AI|ASSISTANT)\]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const excerpt = normalize(evidence);
    if (excerpt.length < 2 || !normalize(turn[1]).includes(excerpt)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: evidence를 해당 신규 RP 턴 원문에서 찾지 못했습니다.`);
  }

  function assertManualLoreQuote(op, draft) {
    if (op.type !== '핵심 대사') return;
    if(draft.recovery&&draft.sourceVerified===false&&draft.explicitUnknownApproval)return;
    const turn = draft.sourceTurns.find(row => row[0] === String(op.sourceTurnKey || ''));
    if (!turn) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 핵심 대사의 sourceTurnKey를 이번 작업 범위에서 찾지 못했습니다.`);
    const quote = String(op.exactQuote || '').trim();
    if (!quote || !String(turn[1]).includes(quote)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 원문 대사가 해당 RP 턴에 연속해서 존재하지 않습니다.`);
    if (!String(op.speaker || '').trim() || !String(op.context || '').trim()) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 핵심 대사의 화자와 맥락이 필요합니다.`);
  }

  function manualLoreDuplicateKey(card) {
    return `${String(card.type || '')}\u0000${String(card.title || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')}`;
  }

  function prepareManualLoreResult(room, draft, values) {
    if (!draft || !manualLoreGuideSlot(room).ready) throw new Error('자료집 지침 미연결 또는 작업자료 없음');
    const raw = String(values.result || '');
    if (raw.length > 600000) throw new Error('자료집 결과가 600,000자를 넘습니다.');
    if (JSON.stringify(manualCheckpointForTask(room, 'lore')) !== JSON.stringify(draft.checkpoint)) throw new Error('자료집 체크포인트가 바뀌었습니다. 작업자료를 다시 만들어 주세요.');
    if (manualLoreGuideSlot(room).text !== draft.guideText) throw new Error('자료집 지침이 바뀌었습니다. 작업자료를 다시 만들어 주세요.');
    if (JSON.stringify(lorePacksFor(room)) !== draft.loreSnapshot) throw new Error('자료집 원본이 바뀌었습니다. 작업자료를 다시 만들어 주세요.');
    const parsed = parseManualLoreResult(raw);
    const candidate = structuredClone(room), baseSnapshot = JSON.stringify(room), refs = draft.loreRefs;
    const packByRef = new Map(), cardByRef = new Map();
    for (const [ref, id] of Object.entries(refs.packIds)) {
      const pack = lorePacksFor(candidate).find(item => String(item.id) === id);
      if (!pack) throw new Error(`${ref}: 기존 자료팩을 찾지 못했습니다.`);
      packByRef.set(ref, pack);
    }
    for (const [ref, identity] of Object.entries(refs.cardIds)) {
      const pack = lorePacksFor(candidate).find(item => String(item.id) === identity.packId);
      const card = (pack?.cards || []).find(item => String(item.id) === identity.cardId);
      if (!card) throw new Error(`${ref}: 기존 자료 카드를 찾지 못했습니다.`);
      cardByRef.set(ref, { pack, card });
    }
    // All references are resolved before a candidate is changed. No guessed ID can pass.
    const newPackRefs = new Set(parsed.operations.filter(op => op.op === 'pack_upsert' && op.ref.startsWith('NEW_')).map(op => op.ref));
    for (const op of parsed.operations) {
      if (op.op === 'pack_upsert' && !op.ref.startsWith('NEW_') && !packByRef.has(op.ref)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 이번 패킷에 없는 자료팩 REF입니다.`);
      if (op.op === 'card_upsert' && !op.ref.startsWith('NEW_') && !cardByRef.has(op.ref)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 이번 패킷에 없는 자료 카드 REF입니다.`);
      if (op.op === 'card_upsert' && !packByRef.has(op.packRef) && !newPackRefs.has(op.packRef)) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 대상 자료팩 ${op.packRef}가 이번 패킷/결과에 없습니다.`);
    }
    const diff = [], counts = { added:0, changed:0, unchanged:0, protected:0, moved:0 };
    const record = (status, op, label, before, after, note = '') => { counts[status]++; diff.push({ status, ref:op.ref, label, before, after, note }); };
    const packOps = parsed.operations.filter(op => op.op === 'pack_upsert');
    const cardOps = parsed.operations.filter(op => op.op === 'card_upsert');
    for (const op of packOps) {
      const old = packByRef.get(op.ref);
      const before = old ? { title:old.title, kind:old.kind, managedBy:lorePackManagedBy(old) } : null;
      const after = { title:op.title.trim(), kind:op.kind };
      if (!old) {
        const duplicate = lorePacksFor(candidate).some(pack => pack.kind === after.kind && String(pack.title || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR') === after.title.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR'));
        if (duplicate) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 같은 제목·종류의 자료팩이 이미 있습니다.`);
        assertManualLoreEvidence(op, draft);
        const pack = { id:makeLoreId('pack'), ...after, enabled:true, managedBy:'ai', cards:[], createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso() };
        candidate.lorePacks = [...lorePacksFor(candidate), pack]; packByRef.set(op.ref, pack);
        record('added', op, `자료팩 · ${after.title}`, null, after);
      } else if (old.title === after.title && old.kind === after.kind) record('unchanged', op, `자료팩 · ${after.title}`, before, after);
      else if (lorePackManagedBy(old) !== 'ai') record('protected', op, `자료팩 · ${after.title}`, before, after, '사용자 관리 자료팩');
      else { assertManualLoreEvidence(op, draft); Object.assign(old, after, { updatedAt:nowIso() }); record('changed', op, `자료팩 · ${after.title}`, before, after); }
    }
    for (const op of cardOps) {
      const targetPack = packByRef.get(op.packRef);
      const old = cardByRef.get(op.ref);
      const opFields = loreCardContentFields(op);
      const oldFields = old ? loreCardContentFields(old.card) : null;
      const fields = parsed.version === 'WISH_LORE_V1' && oldFields ? {
        ...oldFields, ...opFields, entities:oldFields.entities,
        micro:old.card.micro === undefined ? opFields.compact : oldFields.micro,
        exactQuote:oldFields.exactQuote, speaker:oldFields.speaker, target:oldFields.target,
        context:oldFields.context, location:oldFields.location, date:oldFields.date,
      } : opFields;
      const before = old ? { packRef:[...packByRef].find(([,pack]) => pack === old.pack)?.[0] || '', ...loreCardContentFields(old.card), sourceTurnKey:String(old.card.sourceTurnKey || ''), evidence:String(old.card.evidence || '') } : null;
      const sourceTurnKey = String(op.sourceTurnKey || old?.card.sourceTurnKey || '');
      const evidence = String(op.evidence || old?.card.evidence || '');
      const after = { packRef:op.packRef, ...fields, sourceTurnKey, evidence };
      const sameContent = old && JSON.stringify(loreCardContentFields(old.card)) === JSON.stringify(fields) && (!op.sourceTurnKey || String(old.card.sourceTurnKey || '') === op.sourceTurnKey) && (!op.evidence || String(old.card.evidence || '') === op.evidence);
      const samePack = old && old.pack === targetPack;
      if (old && sameContent && samePack) { record('unchanged', op, `카드 · ${fields.title}`, before, after); continue; }
      if (lorePackManagedBy(targetPack) !== 'ai' || (old && loreCardUserLocked(old.card, old.pack))) {
        record('protected', op, `카드 · ${fields.title}`, before, after, old && loreCardUserLocked(old.card, old.pack) ? '사용자 관리/직접 수정 보호 카드' : '사용자 관리 자료팩'); continue;
      }
      const duplicate = (targetPack.cards || []).some(card => card !== old?.card && manualLoreDuplicateKey(card) === manualLoreDuplicateKey(fields));
      if (duplicate) throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 대상 자료팩에 같은 제목·유형 카드가 이미 있습니다.`);
      assertManualLoreEvidence(op, draft);
      if (parsed.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2') assertManualLoreQuote(op, draft);
      if (!old) {
        targetPack.cards.push({ id:makeLoreId('card'), ...fields, sourceTurnKey, evidence, ...LORE_NEW_CARD_DEFAULTS, managedBy:'ai', userLocked:false, createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso() });
        targetPack.updatedAt = nowIso(); record('added', op, `카드 · ${fields.title}`, null, after);
      } else {
        if (!samePack) { old.pack.cards = old.pack.cards.filter(card => card !== old.card); targetPack.cards.push(old.card); old.pack.updatedAt = nowIso(); counts.moved++; }
        Object.assign(old.card, fields, { sourceTurnKey, evidence, updatedAt:nowIso() }); targetPack.updatedAt = nowIso();
        record('changed', op, `카드 · ${fields.title}`, before, after, samePack ? '' : '자료팩 이동');
      }
    }
    const appliedAt = nowIso();
    candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints = { ...(candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints || {}), lore:nextManualCheckpoint(room, 'lore', draft, appliedAt) };
    advanceManualFullBuild(candidate, room, draft);
    candidate.updatedAt = appliedAt;
    const summary = [`추가 ${counts.added}`, `수정 ${counts.changed}`, `변경 없음 ${counts.unchanged}`, `보호로 제외 ${counts.protected}`, `팩 이동 ${counts.moved}`, '삭제 0'];
    return { candidate, baseSnapshot, summary, loreDiff:diff, rawInputs:[raw] };
  }

  async function buildManualLorePacket(room, packetMode = 'standalone', selection = { mode:'checkpoint' }) {
    if(manualBatchRuntime.busy&&!selection.batchCreation)throw new Error('일괄 작업자료 생성이 끝난 뒤 진행해 주세요.');
    const guide = manualLoreGuideSlot(room);
    if (!guide.ready) throw new Error('자료집 지침 미연결 · 최종 지침을 연결한 뒤 작업자료를 만들 수 있습니다.');
    const guideMeta = manualGuideMetadata(room, 'lore', guide);
    const guideBlock = manualPacketGuideBlock(guideMeta, packetMode);
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    if (state.saveStatus === 'saving' || state.saveStatus === 'error') throw new Error('로컬 저장이 완료된 뒤 작업자료를 만들어 주세요.');
    if (selection.mode==='checkpoint'){
      const integrity=await manualIntegrityStatus(room,'lore');
      if(['unverified','mismatch'].includes(integrity.status))throw new Error(`자료집 적용 기록 확인 필요: ${integrity.reason} 전체 RP 또는 직접 범위를 선택해 복구해 주세요.`);
    }
    if (!selection.dryRun) manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(manualPacketKey(room, 'lore'));
    const checkpoint = manualCheckpointForTask(room, 'lore');
    const { turns, fresh, anchorIndex, rangeMode, startIndex, endIndex, checkpointIndex, historyTurns } = await selectManualPacketSource(room, checkpoint, selection);
    const memoBlock = userCanonMemoBlock(room);
    const refs = manualLoreRefs(structuredClone(room));
    const sourceTurns = fresh.map(turn => [String(turn.key), String(turn.text)]);
    const sourceManifest = sourceTurns.map(([sourceTurnKey, text]) => ({ sourceTurnKey, contentHash:simpleHash(text) }));
    const sourceStart = sourceTurns[0][0], sourceEnd = sourceTurns.at(-1)[0];
    const mode = refs.packs.some(pack => pack.managedBy === 'ai') ? 'CONTINUE' : 'INITIAL';
    const resultContract = guide.text.includes('[[WISH_LORE_V2]]') ? MANUAL_LORE_V2_OUTPUT_CONTRACT : MANUAL_LORE_OUTPUT_CONTRACT;
    const packetBase = `[RP_MANAGER_TASK]\nTASK=lore\nMODE=${mode}\nPACKET_MODE=${packetMode}\nSOURCE_RANGE=${rangeMode}\nSOURCE_START=${sourceStart}\nSOURCE_END=${sourceEnd}\nTURN_COUNT=${fresh.length}\nOUTPUT=INCREMENTAL_UPSERT_NO_DELETE\nFACT_SOURCE=THIS_PACKET_ONLY\nROOM_SCOPE=${String(room.chatId || '')}\nGUIDE_NAME=${guideMeta.name}\nGUIDE_VERSION=${guideMeta.version}\nGUIDE_HASH=${guideMeta.hash}\nGUIDE_SOURCE=${guideMeta.source}\n[/RP_MANAGER_TASK]${guideBlock}${memoBlock ? `\n\n${memoBlock}` : ''}\n\n[REF_SNAPSHOT]\n${JSON.stringify({ packs:refs.packs, cards:refs.cards }, null, 2)}\n[/REF_SNAPSHOT]\n\n[SOURCE_MANIFEST]\n${JSON.stringify(sourceManifest, null, 2)}\n[/SOURCE_MANIFEST]\n\n[NEW_RP]\n${sourceTurns.map(([key, text]) => `[RP_TURN sourceTurnKey=${JSON.stringify(key)}]\n${text}\n[/RP_TURN]`).join('\n\n')}\n[/NEW_RP]\n\n[OUTPUT_CONTRACT]\n${resultContract}\n[/OUTPUT_CONTRACT]`;
    const packet = withManualFullBuildNotice(packetBase.replace('[/RP_MANAGER_TASK]',`[/RP_MANAGER_TASK]\n\n${aiMemoryHistoryForPacket(room,'lore',startIndex,endIndex,turns)}`), selection.fullBuild);
    if (packet.length > 600000) throw manualPacketSizeError(packet.length, rangeMode === 'full');
    const draft = { createdAt:Date.now(), startMessageId:sourceStart, endMessageId:sourceEnd, turnCount:fresh.length, sourceManifest, sourceHash:simpleHash(JSON.stringify(sourceManifest)), guideSource:guide.source, guideText:guide.text, guideHash:guideMeta.hash, packetMode, packet, checkpoint, loreRefs:refs, loreSnapshot:JSON.stringify(lorePacksFor(room)), anchorTurn:anchorIndex >= 0 ? [String(turns[anchorIndex].key), String(turns[anchorIndex].text)] : null, sourceTurns, rangeMode, startIndex, endIndex, checkpointIndex, historyTurns, fullBuild:selection.fullBuild || null };
    if (!selection.dryRun) await manualRememberDraft(room, 'lore', draft);
    return { draft, guide };
  }
  const manualUpdateRuntime = { packets:new Map(), counts:new Map(), countLoads:new Set(), commits:new Set(), commitLocks:new Map(), carrierStatuses:new Map() };
  function manualRestoreDrafts(room){
    for(const task of Object.keys(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS))manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(manualPacketKey(room,task));
    for(const [task,job] of Object.entries(room.manualPendingJobs||{})){
      if(!MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task]||!['waiting','waiting-result','result-imported','reviewed','verifying','stale'].includes(job?.status)||!job.draft)continue;
      manualUpdateRuntime.packets.set(manualPacketKey(room,task),job.draft);
    }
  }
  async function manualRememberDraft(room,task,draft){
    const key=manualPacketKey(room,task);
    const previous=room.manualPendingJobs?.[task];
    const previousPacket=manualUpdateRuntime.packets.get(key);
    const jobId=`J_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)}`;
    draft.jobId=jobId;
    draft.memoHash=simpleHash(userCanonMemoBlock(room));
    draft.packet=draft.packet.replace('[RP_MANAGER_TASK]',`[RP_MANAGER_TASK]\nJOB_ID=${jobId}`);
    manualUpdateRuntime.packets.set(key,draft);
    room.manualPendingJobs={...(room.manualPendingJobs||{}),[task]:{
      id:jobId,task,status:'waiting-result',createdAt:nowIso(),
      sourceHash:String(draft.sourceHash||''),startMessageId:String(draft.startMessageId||''),
      endMessageId:String(draft.endMessageId||''),memoHash:draft.memoHash,
      guideHash:String(draft.guideHash||''),packetMode:draft.packetMode,draft
    }};
    try{await saveRoom(room);}catch(error){
      if(previous)room.manualPendingJobs[task]=previous;else delete room.manualPendingJobs[task];
      if(previousPacket)manualUpdateRuntime.packets.set(key,previousPacket);else manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(key);
      throw new Error(`작업자료 대기 상태를 저장하지 못했습니다: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  async function manualPersistImportedResult(room,task,draft,inputs,reviewed=false) {
    if(draft.recovery)return;
    const job=room.manualPendingJobs?.[task];
    if(job?.id===draft.jobId&&job.status==='applied')return;
    if(!job||job.id!==draft.jobId||job.status==='cancelled')throw new Error('진행 중 작업이 바뀌었습니다. 결과를 다시 선택해 주세요.');
    assertManualJobCurrent(room,task,draft,inputs);
    const raw=manualResultInputs(task,inputs).join('\n');
    if(raw.length>600000)throw new Error('결과가 600,000자 안전 한도를 넘습니다.');
    const prior=structuredClone(job);
    job.resultInputs=structuredClone(inputs);
    job.resultMetadata={hash:simpleHash(raw),length:raw.length,updatedAt:nowIso(),verified:reviewed};
    if(job.status!=='stale')job.status=raw.trim()?(reviewed||job.status==='reviewed'&&prior.resultMetadata?.hash===simpleHash(raw)?'reviewed':'result-imported'):'waiting-result';
    noticeReconcile(room);
    const pendingTimer=state.autoSaveTimers.get(String(room.chatId||''));
    if(pendingTimer){clearTimeout(pendingTimer);state.autoSaveTimers.delete(String(room.chatId||''));}
    try{await saveRoom(room);}catch(error){room.manualPendingJobs[task]=prior;throw error;}
  }

  function externalChatTitleInstruction(task) {
    const titles={currentItems:'📋 [현재상태]',currentState:'📋 [현재상태]',logSummary:'🗓️ [날짜요약]',personInfo:'👥 [인물정보]',person:'👥 [인물정보]',timeline:'🕰️ [타임라인]',lore:'📚 [자료집]',first:'🧠 [장기기억 1차]',autoFirst:'🧠 [장기기억 1차]',second:'🧠 [장기기억 2차]',native:'🧠 [장기기억 정리]',integrated:'🔄 [통합 이어갱신]',rebuild:'🧩 [전체 재구축]',compression:'📦 [자료 압축]'};
    const title=titles[task];
    return title ? `[EXTERNAL_CHAT_TITLE]\n이 작업으로 새 채팅의 제목을 정할 수 있다면 아래 이름을 그대로 사용한다.\n${title}\n‘전체본 완성’, ‘업데이트 결과’ 같은 부가 문구를 붙이지 않는다. 제목을 직접 설정할 수 없는 환경에서는 작업만 수행한다. 이 요청은 채팅 이름에만 적용하며 결과 본문·JSON·카드 제목·파일명에는 추가하지 않는다. 출력 형식과 검증 규칙을 그대로 따른다.\n[/EXTERNAL_CHAT_TITLE]` : '';
  }

  function manualGuideForTask(room, task) {
    if (task === 'lore') return manualLoreGuideSlot(room);
    if (task === 'currentItems' && isGuideTextModified('currentState')) return { text:getGuideText('currentState'), source:'사용자 수정 지침' };
    if (task === 'logSummary') {
      const variant = getLogSummaryGuideVariant(false);
      if (isGuideTextModified('logSummary', variant)) return { text:getGuideText('logSummary', variant), source:`사용자 수정 지침 · ${variant === 'adult' ? '성인용' : '일반용'}` };
    }
    if (task === 'personInfo' && isPersonGuideModified(room)) return { text:effectivePersonGuide(room), source:'이 방의 사용자 수정 지침' };
    if (task === 'timeline' && isStoryTimelineGuideModified(room)) return { text:effectiveTimelineGuide(room), source:'이 방의 사용자 수정 지침' };
    return { text:canonicalGuideText(task), source:`기본 지침 ${MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].version}` };
  }

  function manualGuideMetadata(room, task, guide = manualGuideForTask(room, task)) {
    const text = String(guide.text || '');
    const firstLine = text.split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0].trim();
    const custom = /사용자/.test(String(guide.source || ''));
    return {
      task,
      name:firstLine || MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title,
      version:custom ? (firstLine.match(/\bv\d+(?:\.\d+)+\b/i)?.[0] || '사용자 수정본') : MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].version,
      hash:`fnv1a32:${simpleHash(text)}:${text.length}`,
      source:String(guide.source || ''),
      text,
    };
  }

  function manualGuideOnlyText(room, task) {
    const guide = manualGuideMetadata(room, task);
    return `${externalChatTitleInstruction(task)}\n\n[RP_MANAGER_GUIDE]\nTASK=${task}\nGUIDE_NAME=${guide.name}\nGUIDE_VERSION=${guide.version}\nGUIDE_HASH=${guide.hash}\nGUIDE_SOURCE=${guide.source}\n[/RP_MANAGER_GUIDE]\n\n[GUIDE]\n${guide.text}\n[/GUIDE]`;
  }

  function manualPacketGuideBlock(guide, packetMode) {
    if (!['light','standalone'].includes(packetMode)) throw new Error('작업자료 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.');
    return `\n\n${externalChatTitleInstruction(guide.task)}` + (packetMode === 'standalone' ? `\n\n[GUIDE]\n${guide.text}\n[/GUIDE]` : '');
  }

  function manualPacketSizeError(length, fullRange) {
    const error = new Error(`작업자료 ${formatCount(length)}자가 안전 표시 한도(600,000자)를 넘었습니다. 원문은 자르지 않았습니다.`);
    if (fullRange) error.code = 'RPCM_FULL_SPLIT_REQUIRED';
    return error;
  }

  function withManualFullBuildNotice(packet, part) {
    if (!part) return packet;
    const metadata = [
      'BUILD_SCOPE=FULL_SPLIT',
      `BUILD_PART=${part.part}`,
      `BUILD_PART_ESTIMATE=${part.estimatedParts}`,
      `BUILD_TOTAL_TURNS=${part.totalTurns}`,
      `BUILD_PART_START_TURN=${part.startIndex + 1}`,
      `BUILD_PART_END_TURN=${part.endIndex + 1}`,
      `BUILD_MORE_EXPECTED=${part.endIndex < part.totalTurns - 1 ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`,
    ].join('\n');
    const notice = `[SPLIT_BUILD_NOTICE]
이 작업은 전체 RP를 순차 분할하여 구축하는 현재 구간입니다.
이번 구간은 지금 정상 처리하고 지침에 맞는 결과를 출력하세요.
이번 결과를 RP Manager에 저장한 뒤, 최신 저장 결과와 REF를 포함한 다음 구간 작업자료를 별도로 생성합니다.
현재 패킷에 없는 뒤 구간의 사실을 추측하거나 미리 반영하지 마세요.
[/SPLIT_BUILD_NOTICE]`;
    return packet.replace('[/RP_MANAGER_TASK]', `${metadata}\n[/RP_MANAGER_TASK]`) + `\n\n${notice}`;
  }

  function manualCheckpointForTask(room, task) {
    if (task === 'timeline') return { endMessageId:String(room.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId || ''), appliedAt:String(room.storyTimelineLastReviewedAt || ''), baselineAt:String(room.manualUpdateCheckpoints?.timeline?.baselineAt || (String(room.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId || '') === String(room.autoStartBaseline?.turnKey || '') ? room.autoStartBaseline?.setAt || '' : '')) };
    const saved = room.manualUpdateCheckpoints?.[task];
    return { endMessageId:String(saved?.endMessageId || ''), appliedAt:String(saved?.appliedAt || ''), baselineAt:String(saved?.baselineAt || '') };
  }

  // Cursor pages are consumed newest first; only one unfinished turn crosses a page.
  // Full-source callers retain completed turn chunks, not thousands of raw API objects.
  function createRpcmTurnPager(room){
    const chatId=String(apiChatIdOf(room)),seenCursors=new Set(),seenMessages=new Map();
    let cursor='',carry=[],done=false,messageCount=0,pageCount=0;
    return {get done(){return done;},get messageCount(){return messageCount;},async next(){
      if(done)return [];
      if(state.currentRoom!==room||String(apiChatIdOf(room))!==chatId)throw new Error('RP 조회 중 방 또는 분기가 바뀌었습니다.');
      const url=`https://crack-api.wrtn.ai/crack-gen/v3/chats/${chatId}/messages?limit=50${cursor?'&cursor='+encodeURIComponent(cursor):''}`;
      const data=await apiRequest('GET',url),raw=data?.data?.messages||data?.messages;
      if(!Array.isArray(raw))throw new Error('RP 페이지의 메시지 형식이 올바르지 않습니다. 전체 조회 완료로 처리하지 않았습니다.');
      if(state.currentRoom!==room||String(apiChatIdOf(room))!==chatId)throw new Error('RP 조회 중 방 또는 분기가 바뀌었습니다.');
      const page=[];
      for(const message of raw){const id=String(messageIdOf(message)||''),signature=semanticSourceHash(JSON.stringify([messageRoleOf(message),messageTextOf(message),message.turnId,message.status,message.generationStatus]));if(id&&seenMessages.has(id)){if(seenMessages.get(id)!==signature)throw new Error('페이지 조회 도중 RP 원문이 바뀌었습니다. 다시 조회해 주세요.');continue;}if(id)seenMessages.set(id,signature);page.push(message);}
      messageCount+=page.length;pageCount++;
      const next=String(data?.data?.nextCursor||data?.nextCursor||'');
      if(next&&(next===cursor||seenCursors.has(next)))throw new Error('RP 페이지 cursor가 반복되었습니다. 일부 기록을 전체로 처리하지 않았습니다.');
      if(next){seenCursors.add(next);cursor=next;}else done=true;
      if(next&&!raw.length)throw new Error('빈 RP 페이지에 다음 cursor가 있습니다. 전체 원문을 확인하지 못했습니다.');
      const combined=page.reverse().concat(carry),firstUser=combined.findIndex(row=>messageRoleOf(row)==='user'&&stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(row),true).trim());
      const turns=firstUser<0?[]:buildCompletedAiDialogueTurns(combined.slice(firstUser));
      carry=firstUser<0?combined:combined.slice(0,firstUser);
      if(carry.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+messageTextOf(row).length,0)>2000000)throw new Error('하나의 RP 턴이 안전 메모리 크기를 넘습니다. 원문을 자르지 않았습니다.');
      if(done)carry=[];
      const status=state.modal?.querySelector('[data-rebuild-status]');if(status&&fullRebuildRuntime.busy)status.textContent=`원문 ${formatCount(messageCount)}개 · ${pageCount}페이지 조회 ${done?'완료':'중 (전체 확인 전)'}`;
      return turns;
    }};
  }
  async function loadRpcmCompletedRange(room,anchorKeys=[],options={}){
    const pager=createRpcmTurnPager(room),anchors=[...new Set(anchorKeys.map(String).filter(Boolean))],remaining=new Set(anchors),chunks=[];
    while(!pager.done){
      const batch=await pager.next();chunks.push(batch);
      for(const turn of batch)for(const alias of [turn.key,...(turn.aliases||[])])remaining.delete(String(alias));
      if(anchors.length&&!remaining.size){const ordered=chunks.slice().reverse().flat(),earliest=Math.min(...anchors.map(key=>rpcmFindTurnIndex(ordered,key)));if(earliest>=Number(options.lookbackTurns||0))break;}
      if(options.recentOnly&&chunks.length>=3&&!options.recentTurns)break;
      if(options.recentOnly&&batch.length&&chunks.reduce((n,part)=>n+part.length,0)>=Number(options.recentTurns||1))break;
    }
    if(remaining.size&&!options.allowMissingAnchor)throw new Error('기준 RP가 사라졌습니다. 리롤·수정·삭제·분기를 확인하고 시작점을 다시 설정해 주세요.');
    const turns=chunks.reverse().flat();
    if(options.recentOnly&&!turns.length)throw new Error('최근 메시지에서 정상 완료 USER+AI 턴을 확정하지 못했습니다. 응답 완료 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    return turns;
  }
  async function loadLatestCompletedTurns(room){return loadRpcmCompletedRange(room,[],{recentOnly:true});}
  function aiMemoryIgnoredManualAnchor(room,area){const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area],record=room.aiMemorySourceAnchors?.[area],key=manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual).endMessageId;return record&&record.key===String(row.cursor||row.baseline)&&record.supersededCheckpoint===String(key||'')?key:'';}
  function aiMemoryScopeAnchors(room,areas){
    const keys=[];
    for(const area of areas){const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];if(!row)continue;const key=row.cursor||row.baseline;if(key)keys.push(key);const checkpoint=manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual).endMessageId;if(checkpoint&&checkpoint!==aiMemoryIgnoredManualAnchor(room,area))keys.push(checkpoint);}
    return [...new Set(keys)];
  }
  function aiMemoryAnchorFingerprint(turn){return semanticSourceHash(JSON.stringify([turn.key,turn.text,(turn.reviewSources||[]).map(row=>row.id)]));}
  function aiMemorySetAnchorEvidence(room,area,turn,ignoreCheckpoint=false){
    room.aiMemorySourceAnchors||={};if(turn)room.aiMemorySourceAnchors[area]={key:String(turn.key),hash:aiMemoryAnchorFingerprint(turn),chatId:String(apiChatIdOf(room)),...(ignoreCheckpoint?{supersededCheckpoint:String(manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual).endMessageId||'')}:{})};else delete room.aiMemorySourceAnchors[area];
  }
  function aiMemoryCheckAnchorEvidence(room,areas,turns){
    for(const area of areas){const record=room.aiMemorySourceAnchors?.[area],row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];if(!record||record.key!==String(row.cursor||row.baseline))continue;const turn=turns[rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,record.key)];if(!turn||record.chatId!==String(apiChatIdOf(room))||record.hash!==aiMemoryAnchorFingerprint(turn))throw new Error(`${AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label}: 기준 RP의 원문·리롤·분기가 바뀌었습니다. 시작점을 다시 설정해 주세요.`);}
  }
  async function aiMemoryScopedTurns(room,areas){
    const anchors=aiMemoryScopeAnchors(room,areas),needsBeginning=areas.some(area=>!aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area].cursor&&!aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area].baseline&&!manualCheckpointForTask(room,AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].manual).endMessageId);
    const turns=await loadRpcmCompletedRange(room,needsBeginning?[]:anchors);aiMemoryCheckAnchorEvidence(room,areas,turns);return turns;
  }
  async function aiMemoryStartFromNow(room,area){
    const turns=await loadLatestCompletedTurns(room),turn=turns.at(-1),row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area];
    if(state.currentRoom!==room)throw new Error('방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    const previous=structuredClone(row),anchors=structuredClone(room.aiMemorySourceAnchors||{});
    Object.assign(row,{baseline:String(turn.key),cursor:'',draft:null,status:'지금부터 대기',error:'',failedEnd:'',plannedStart:'',plannedEnd:''});
    aiMemorySetAnchorEvidence(room,area,turn,true);
    try{await saveRoom(room);}catch(error){Object.assign(row,previous);room.aiMemorySourceAnchors=anchors;throw error;}
    invalidateAiMemoryHistory();const key=String(room.chatId),counts=aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.get(key)||{};aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.set(key,{...counts,[area]:0,lastKey:String(turn.key)});
    return turn;
  }
  async function openAiMemoryBaselineDialog(room,area){
    document.getElementById('rpcm-ai-memory-baseline-dialog')?.remove();
    const row=aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area],pager=createRpcmTurnPager(room),box=document.createElement('div');box.id='rpcm-ai-memory-baseline-dialog';
    box.innerHTML=`<section role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="AI 기억정리 시작점"><h3>${esc(AI_MEMORY_AREAS[area].label)} 시작점</h3><p>최근 완료 턴부터 표시합니다. 선택한 턴 다음부터 자동 생성합니다.</p><select aria-label="시작점 RP"><option value="">처음부터</option></select><p data-status role="status">최근 RP 조회 중…</p><button type="button" data-older>이전 턴 더 불러오기</button><div><button type="button" data-close>취소</button><button type="button" data-save disabled>시작점 저장</button></div></section>`;
    document.body.appendChild(box);let loaded=[],busy=false;
    box.querySelector('[data-close]').onclick=()=>box.remove();
    const more=async()=>{if(busy||pager.done)return;busy=true;box.querySelector('[data-older]').disabled=true;try{let batch=[];for(let i=0;i<3&&!batch.length&&!pager.done;i++)batch=await pager.next();if(!box.isConnected)return;loaded=batch.concat(loaded);const select=box.querySelector('select'),chosen=select.dataset.loaded?select.value:row.baseline;select.dataset.loaded='1';const visible=loaded.slice(0,200);const existing=loaded.find(turn=>String(turn.key)===chosen);if(existing&&!visible.includes(existing))visible.push(existing);select.innerHTML='<option value="">처음부터</option>'+visible.slice().reverse().map(turn=>`<option value="${esc(String(turn.key))}">${esc('USER '+turn.userText.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,42)+' / AI '+turn.assistantText.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,42))}</option>`).join('');if(visible.some(turn=>String(turn.key)===chosen))select.value=chosen;else if(!chosen&&!row.baseline)select.value=String(visible.at(-1)?.key||'');else if(chosen){select.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',`<option value="${esc(chosen)}" disabled>기존 시작점 · 이전 턴을 불러와 확인하세요</option>`);select.value=chosen;}box.querySelector('[data-status]').textContent=`완료 턴 ${loaded.length}개 조회 · 선택 목록 최대 200개${pager.done?' · 원문 시작 도달':''}`;box.querySelector('[data-save]').disabled=false;}catch(error){box.querySelector('[data-status]').textContent=error.message;box.querySelector('[data-save]').disabled=true;box.dataset.loadError='1';}finally{busy=false;if(box.isConnected)box.querySelector('[data-older]').disabled=pager.done||box.dataset.loadError==='1';}};
    box.querySelector('[data-older]').onclick=more;
    box.querySelector('[data-save]').onclick=async event=>{if(row.draft&&!confirm('검토 대기 초안을 버리고 시작점을 바꿀까요?'))return;event.currentTarget.disabled=true;try{const key=box.querySelector('select').value,selected=loaded.find(turn=>String(turn.key)===key);if(key&&!selected)throw new Error('선택한 RP를 다시 불러와 주세요.');if(key){const check=await loadRpcmCompletedRange(room,[key]);if(aiMemoryAnchorFingerprint(check[rpcmFindTurnIndex(check,key)])!==aiMemoryAnchorFingerprint(selected))throw new Error('선택한 RP 원문이 바뀌었습니다. 다시 열어 주세요.');}const previous=structuredClone(row),anchors=structuredClone(room.aiMemorySourceAnchors||{});Object.assign(row,{baseline:key,cursor:'',failedEnd:'',plannedStart:'',plannedEnd:'',draft:null,error:'',status:'시작점 지정됨'});aiMemorySetAnchorEvidence(room,area,selected);try{await saveRoom(room);}catch(error){Object.assign(row,previous);room.aiMemorySourceAnchors=anchors;throw error;}box.remove();invalidateAiMemoryHistory();aiMemoryRuntime.lastCounts.delete(String(room.chatId));renderModalIfOpen();void aiMemoryPoll(room,true);}catch(error){box.querySelector('[data-status]').textContent=error.message;event.currentTarget.disabled=false;}};
    await more();
  }

  async function loadManualCompletedTurns(room) {
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    const turns=await loadRpcmCompletedRange(room);personCollectAppearances(room,turns);return turns;
  }

  function manualTurnOption(turn, index) {
    const short = text => String(text || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 42);
    return `${index + 1} · USER ${short(turn.userText)} / AI ${short(turn.assistantText)}`;
  }

  async function selectManualPacketSource(room, checkpoint, selection = { mode:'checkpoint' }) {
    const rangeMode = String(selection?.mode || 'checkpoint');
    if (!['checkpoint','full','custom'].includes(rangeMode)) throw new Error('RP 범위 선택이 올바르지 않습니다.');
    let turns, anchorIndex = -1, historyTurns = null;
    if (rangeMode === 'checkpoint') {
      turns = selection.loadedTurns || await loadRpcmCompletedRange(room,checkpoint.endMessageId?[checkpoint.endMessageId]:[]);
      anchorIndex = checkpoint.endMessageId ? rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,checkpoint.endMessageId) : -1;
      if (checkpoint.endMessageId && anchorIndex < 0) throw new Error('마지막 적용 RP를 찾지 못했습니다. 리롤·분기 또는 조회 한도를 확인해 주세요.');

    } else {
      turns = selection.loadedTurns || await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
      historyTurns = turns.map(turn => [String(turn.key), String(turn.text)]);
      anchorIndex = checkpoint.endMessageId ? rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,checkpoint.endMessageId) : -1;
      if (checkpoint.endMessageId && anchorIndex < 0 && checkpoint.endMessageId!==selection.priorCheckpoint) throw new Error('기존 기준 RP를 전체 기록에서 찾지 못했습니다. 체크포인트 진행 방향을 안전하게 판정할 수 없습니다.');
    }
    let startIndex = anchorIndex + 1, endIndex = turns.length - 1;
    if (rangeMode === 'full') startIndex = 0;
    if (rangeMode === 'custom') {
      startIndex = rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,String(selection.startKey || ''));
      endIndex = rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,String(selection.endKey || ''));
      if (startIndex < 0 || endIndex < 0 || startIndex > endIndex) throw new Error('시작·종료 RP를 다시 선택해 주세요. 종료는 시작보다 앞설 수 없습니다.');
    }
    const fresh = turns.slice(startIndex, endIndex + 1);
    if (!fresh.length) throw new Error(rangeMode === 'checkpoint' ? '기준점 이후 완료된 새 RP 턴이 없습니다.' : '선택한 범위에 완료된 RP 턴이 없습니다.');
    return { rangeMode, turns, fresh, anchorIndex, startIndex, endIndex, historyTurns, checkpointIndex:anchorIndex };
  }

  function nextManualCheckpoint(room, task, draft, appliedAt) {
    const current = room.manualUpdateCheckpoints?.[task] || {};
    const forward = manualRangeMovesForward(draft);
    if (!forward) return { ...current };
    return { ...current, startMessageId:draft.startMessageId, endMessageId:draft.endMessageId, turnCount:draft.turnCount, appliedAt, ...(task === 'lore' ? { sourceHash:draft.sourceHash } : {}) };
  }

  function manualRangeMovesForward(draft) {
    if(draft.recovery)return false;
    if (draft.fullBuild && draft.fullBuild.endIndex < draft.fullBuild.totalTurns - 1) return false;
    return draft.rangeMode === 'checkpoint' || draft.checkpointIndex < 0 || draft.endIndex > draft.checkpointIndex;
  }

  function manualFullBuildFor(room, task) { return room?.manualFullBuilds?.[task] || null; }

  async function manualFullHistoryHash(turns) {
    const source = JSON.stringify(turns.map(turn => [String(turn.key), String(turn.text)]));
    const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(source));
    return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map(byte => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  }

  async function assertManualFullBuildHistory(room, task, progress) {
    const turns = await loadManualCompletedTurns(room,{finalFresh:true});
    const scope=turns.slice(0,progress.totalTurns);
    const hash = await manualFullHistoryHash(scope);
    if (scope.length !== progress.totalTurns || String(turns[0]?.key || '') !== progress.startKey || String(scope.at(-1)?.key || '') !== progress.endKey || hash !== progress.historyHash) {
      throw new Error('RP 원문이 변경되었습니다. 전체 구축 범위를 다시 확인해 주세요. 기존 저장 결과는 유지됩니다.');
    }
    if (manualFullBuildFor(room, task)?.historyHash !== progress.historyHash) throw new Error('전체 구축 진행 상태가 바뀌었습니다. 다시 확인해 주세요.');
    return scope;
  }

  async function planManualFullBuildPart(room, task, packetMode, turns, startIndex) {
    if (startIndex < 0 || startIndex >= turns.length) throw new Error('처리할 전체 구축 RP 범위가 없습니다.');
    const startKey = String(turns[startIndex].key);
    let low = startIndex, high = turns.length - 1, best = null;
    while (low <= high) {
      const mid = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
      let candidate = null;
      try { candidate = await buildManualUpdatePacket(room, task, packetMode, { mode:'custom', startKey, endKey:String(turns[mid].key), loadedTurns:turns, dryRun:true }); }
      catch (error) { if (!/안전 표시 한도/.test(error.message)) throw error; }
      // Leave room for the final split metadata and notice added after planning.
      if (candidate && candidate.draft.packet.length <= 495000) { best = { endIndex:mid, length:candidate.draft.packet.length }; low = mid + 1; }
      else high = mid - 1;
    }
    if (!best) throw new Error('지침·기존 결과·단일 RP 턴만으로 안전 목표 500,000자를 넘습니다. 자동 분할할 수 없어 직접 범위를 확인해야 합니다.');
    return { startIndex, endIndex:best.endIndex, packetLength:best.length };
  }

  async function makeManualFullBuildPart(room, task, packetMode, startNew = false) {
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    const roomKey = String(room.chatId || '');
    if (manualUpdateRuntime.commits.has(roomKey) || state.autoSaveTimers.has(roomKey) || state.saveStatus === 'saving' || state.saveStatus === 'error') throw new Error('로컬 저장이 완료된 뒤 전체 구축을 진행해 주세요.');
    const before = JSON.stringify(room);
    let progress = manualFullBuildFor(room, task);
    if (startNew && progress) throw new Error('진행 중인 전체 구축을 먼저 이어가거나 취소해 주세요.');
    if (!startNew && !progress) throw new Error('이어갈 전체 구축 진행 상태가 없습니다.');
    const turns = startNew ? await loadManualCompletedTurns(room) : await assertManualFullBuildHistory(room, task, progress);
    if (JSON.stringify(room) !== before) throw new Error('RP 조회 중 저장 데이터가 바뀌었습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    if (!turns.length) throw new Error('완료된 RP 턴이 없습니다.');
    const historyHash = startNew ? await manualFullHistoryHash(turns) : progress.historyHash;
    const startIndex = startNew ? 0 : progress.lastAppliedIndex + 1;
    const plan = await planManualFullBuildPart(room, task, packetMode, turns, startIndex);
    if (startNew) {
      progress = { task, startKey:String(turns[0].key), endKey:String(turns.at(-1).key), totalTurns:turns.length, lastAppliedEndKey:'', lastAppliedIndex:-1, completedParts:0, startedAt:nowIso(), historyHash, packetMode, estimatedParts:Math.ceil(turns.length / (plan.endIndex + 1)) };
      const candidate = structuredClone(room);
      candidate.manualFullBuilds = { ...(candidate.manualFullBuilds || {}), [task]:progress };
      if (candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted) {
        delete candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted[task];
        if (!Object.keys(candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted).length) delete candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted;
      }
      if (JSON.stringify(room) !== before) throw new Error('전체 구축 시작 전 저장 데이터가 바뀌었습니다.');
      await saveRoom(candidate);
      if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('저장 중 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
      rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,candidate);
      if (!candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted) delete room.manualFullBuildCompleted;
    }
    const fullBuild = { task, historyHash, startIndex:plan.startIndex, endIndex:plan.endIndex, totalTurns:turns.length, part:progress.completedParts + 1, estimatedParts:progress.estimatedParts };
    const selection = { mode:'custom', startKey:String(turns[plan.startIndex].key), endKey:String(turns[plan.endIndex].key), loadedTurns:turns, fullBuild };
    const result = await buildManualUpdatePacket(room, task, packetMode, selection);
    if (result.draft.packet.length > 500000) throw new Error('최종 작업자료가 안전 목표를 넘었습니다. 전체 구축 진행 상태를 취소하고 범위를 확인해 주세요.');
    return { ...result, part:progress.completedParts + 1, totalTurns:turns.length, startIndex:plan.startIndex, endIndex:plan.endIndex, estimatedParts:progress.estimatedParts };
  }

  function advanceManualFullBuild(candidate, room, draft) {
    const part = draft.fullBuild;
    if (!part) return;
    const progress = manualFullBuildFor(room, part.task);
    if (!progress || progress.historyHash !== part.historyHash || progress.totalTurns !== part.totalTurns || progress.lastAppliedIndex + 1 !== part.startIndex || part.endIndex !== draft.endIndex) throw new Error('전체 구축 진행 상태가 변경됐습니다. 작업자료를 다시 만들어 주세요.');
    candidate.manualFullBuilds = { ...(candidate.manualFullBuilds || {}) };
    if (part.endIndex >= progress.totalTurns - 1) {
      delete candidate.manualFullBuilds[part.task];
      candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted = { ...(candidate.manualFullBuildCompleted || {}), [part.task]:{ totalTurns:progress.totalTurns, completedParts:progress.completedParts + 1, endKey:draft.endMessageId, completedAt:nowIso() } };
    }
    else candidate.manualFullBuilds[part.task] = { ...progress, lastAppliedIndex:part.endIndex, lastAppliedEndKey:draft.endMessageId, completedParts:progress.completedParts + 1, partRanges:[...(progress.partRanges||[]),{part:part.part,startIndex:part.startIndex,endIndex:part.endIndex}] };
    if (!Object.keys(candidate.manualFullBuilds).length) delete candidate.manualFullBuilds;
  }

  async function cancelManualFullBuild(room, task) {
    const progress = manualFullBuildFor(room, task);
    if (!progress) return;
    const roomKey = String(room.chatId || '');
    if (manualUpdateRuntime.commits.has(roomKey) || state.autoSaveTimers.has(roomKey)) throw new Error('저장이 끝난 뒤 전체 구축을 취소해 주세요.');
    const candidate = structuredClone(room);
    candidate.manualFullBuilds = { ...(candidate.manualFullBuilds || {}) };
    delete candidate.manualFullBuilds[task];
    if (!Object.keys(candidate.manualFullBuilds).length) delete candidate.manualFullBuilds;
    await saveRoom(candidate);
    rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,candidate);
    if (!candidate.manualFullBuilds) delete room.manualFullBuilds;
    manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(manualPacketKey(room, task));
    refreshManualUpdateCards(state.modal, room);
  }

  async function setManualTaskStartpoint(room, mode, target, selectedKey = '') {
    if (!room || state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    const roomKey = String(room.chatId || '');
    if (manualUpdateRuntime.commits.has(roomKey) || manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.has(roomKey) || state.autoSaveTimers.has(roomKey) || state.saveStatus === 'saving' || state.saveStatus === 'error') throw new Error('기존 로컬 저장이 완료된 뒤 시작점을 설정해 주세요.');
    const baseSnapshot = JSON.stringify(room);
    if (!['now','beginning','selected'].includes(mode)) throw new Error('시작점 방식을 선택해 주세요.');
    const includeLongTerm = target === 'all' || target === 'longTermFirst';
    const targets = target === 'all' ? Object.keys(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS) : target === 'longTermFirst' ? [] : [target];
    if ((!targets.length && !includeLongTerm) || targets.some(task => !MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task])) throw new Error('적용할 작업을 선택해 주세요.');
    const turns = await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
    if (manualUpdateRuntime.commits.has(roomKey) || manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.has(roomKey) || state.autoSaveTimers.has(roomKey) || JSON.stringify(room) !== baseSnapshot) throw new Error('RP 조회 중 저장 데이터가 바뀌었습니다. 시작점을 다시 확인해 주세요.');
    const index = mode === 'selected' ? rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,selectedKey) : -1;
    if (mode === 'selected' && index < 0) throw new Error('선택한 RP 턴을 찾지 못했습니다. 목록을 다시 불러와 주세요.');
    if (mode === 'now' && !turns.length) throw new Error('완료된 RP 턴이 없어 지금부터로 설정할 수 없습니다.');
    const key = mode === 'now' ? String(turns.at(-1).key) : mode === 'selected' && index > 0 ? String(turns[index - 1].key) : '';
    const now = nowIso();
    const candidate = structuredClone(room);
    candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints = { ...(candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints || {}) };
    for (const task of targets) {
      candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints[task] = { ...(candidate.manualUpdateCheckpoints[task] || {}), endMessageId:key, baselineAt:now };
      if (task === 'timeline') {
        candidate.storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId = key;
        const checkpointIndex = key ? rpcmFindTurnIndex(turns,key) : -1;
        candidate.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount = turns.length - checkpointIndex - 1;
        candidate.storyTimelineCountCheckedAt = Date.now();
        candidate.storyTimelineCarryoverTurnKeys = checkpointIndex < 0 ? [] : turns.slice(Math.max(0, checkpointIndex - 39), checkpointIndex + 1).map(turn => String(turn.key));
      }
    }
    let clearedLongTermPending = 0;
    if (includeLongTerm) {
      const longTerm = longTermState(candidate);
      const previousBaseline = String(longTerm.firstStage.manualBaseline || '');
      longTerm.firstStage.manualBaseline = key;
      if (previousBaseline !== key) clearedLongTermPending = longTermClearManualPending(candidate);
    }
    await saveRoom(candidate);
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('저장 중 채팅방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,candidate);
    manualUpdateRuntime.counts.delete(roomKey);
    for (const task of targets) manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(manualPacketKey(room, task));
    if (includeLongTerm && clearedLongTermPending) {
      longTermRuntime.externalText = '';
      if (longTermRuntime.draftView === 'first') longTermRuntime.draftView = '';
    }
    refreshManualUpdateCards(state.modal, room);
    void refreshManualUpdateCounts(state.modal, room, true);
    return { count:targets.length + (includeLongTerm ? 1 : 0), mode, key, clearedLongTermPending };
  }

  function openAutoStartDialog(room) {
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-start-backdrop')?.remove();
    const previousFocus = document.activeElement;
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
    backdrop.id = 'rpcm-auto-start-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML = `<section class="rpcm-auto-start-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="rpcm-auto-start-title"><h2 id="rpcm-auto-start-title">시작점 설정</h2><p>선택한 작업의 다음 기본 업데이트 범위만 바꿉니다. 이미 저장된 기억은 유지되며, 시작점이 바뀐 작업의 미저장 작업자료·결과 대기는 취소됩니다.</p><label>시작 방식 <select data-start-mode><option value="" selected disabled>방식 선택</option><option value="now">지금부터</option><option value="beginning">처음부터</option><option value="selected">특정 RP부터</option></select></label><label>적용 대상 <select data-start-target><option value="" selected disabled>작업 선택</option><option value="all">전체 6작업</option>${Object.entries(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS).map(([task, info]) => `<option value="${task}">${esc(info.title)}</option>`).join('')}<option value="longTermFirst">장기기억 1차 생성</option></select></label><label data-start-turn-wrap hidden>시작할 RP 턴 <select data-start-turn></select></label><p data-start-note>특정 RP부터를 선택하면 선택한 턴을 다음 작업자료에 포함합니다.</p><footer><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-auto-start-cancel>취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-auto-start-confirm>시작점 저장</button></footer></section>`;
    let loadedTurns = null;
    const modeSelect = backdrop.querySelector('[data-start-mode]');
    const targetSelect = backdrop.querySelector('[data-start-target]');
    const turnSelect = backdrop.querySelector('[data-start-turn]');
    modeSelect.addEventListener('change', async () => {
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-start-turn-wrap]').hidden = modeSelect.value !== 'selected';
      if (modeSelect.value !== 'selected' || loadedTurns) return;
      const note = backdrop.querySelector('[data-start-note]');
      note.textContent = '완료된 RP 턴을 불러오는 중…';
      try {
        loadedTurns = await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
        if (!backdrop.isConnected) return;
        if (!loadedTurns.length) throw new Error('완료된 RP 턴이 없습니다.');
        turnSelect.innerHTML = loadedTurns.map((turn, index) => `<option value="${esc(String(turn.key))}">${esc(manualTurnOption(turn, index))}</option>`).join('');
        note.textContent = '선택한 턴부터 다음 작업자료에 포함합니다.';
      } catch (error) { loadedTurns = null; note.textContent = `목록 조회 실패: ${error.message}`; }
    });
    const close = () => { backdrop.remove(); if (previousFocus?.isConnected) previousFocus.focus(); };
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-auto-start-cancel]').onclick = close;
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-auto-start-confirm]').onclick = async event => {
      const button = event.currentTarget;
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = '시작점 확인 중…';
      try {
        if (!modeSelect.value || !targetSelect.value) throw new Error('시작 방식과 적용 대상을 선택해 주세요.');
        if (modeSelect.value === 'selected' && !turnSelect.value) throw new Error('시작할 RP 턴을 선택해 주세요.');
        const result = await setManualTaskStartpoint(room, modeSelect.value, targetSelect.value, turnSelect.value);
        close();
        const label = state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-start-status');
        if (label) label.textContent = `${result.count}개 작업 시작점 저장됨 · ${new Date().toLocaleString('ko-KR')}`;
        notify(`${result.count}개 작업의 시작점을 저장했습니다.${result.clearedLongTermPending ? ' 장기기억 1차의 이전 결과 대기도 정리했습니다.' : ''} 저장된 기억은 유지됩니다.`, 'success', 5000);
      } catch (error) {
        notify(`시작점을 변경하지 않았습니다: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6500);
        button.disabled = false;
        button.textContent = '시작점 저장';
      }
    };
    backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(); };
    backdrop.onkeydown = event => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') { event.preventDefault(); close(); }
      else if (event.key === 'Tab') {
        const buttons = [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled)')];
        if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === buttons[0]) { event.preventDefault(); buttons.at(-1).focus(); }
        else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === buttons.at(-1)) { event.preventDefault(); buttons[0].focus(); }
      }
    };
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-auto-start-cancel]').focus();
  }

  function manualExistingResult(room, task, timelineRun = null) {
    const slot = id => String((room.slots || []).find(item => item.id === id)?.content || '').trim();
    if (task === 'currentItems') return `[CURRENT_STATE]\n${slot('currentState')}\n[/CURRENT_STATE]\n\n[IMPORTANT_ITEMS]\n${allImportantItemRawText(room).trim()}\n[/IMPORTANT_ITEMS]`;
    if (task === 'logSummary') return `[DATE_LOGS]\n${slot('logSummary')}\n[/DATE_LOGS]`;
    if (task === 'personInfo') {
      const rows = personStateExportText(room).split('\n').map(line => {
        if (!line.startsWith('{')) return line;
        try { return JSON.stringify({ op:'upsert', ...JSON.parse(line) }); } catch (_) { return line; }
      });
      return rows.join('\n');
    }
    return serializeStoryTimelineRefMaster(timelineRun.masterSnapshot, timelineRun, true);
  }

  function manualReferenceData(room, task) {
    const current = String((room.slots || []).find(slot => slot.id === 'currentState')?.content || '').trim();
    if (task === 'logSummary' || task === 'personInfo' || task === 'timeline') return `[CURRENT_STATE]\n${current}\n[/CURRENT_STATE]`;
    return '별도 참고자료 없음. 사실 근거는 기존 결과와 이번 신규 RP에 한정합니다.';
  }

  function manualOutputContract(task) {
    if (task === 'currentItems') return '현재상태.txt와 중요물품.txt에 대응하는 본문 두 개를 분리해 전체 출력합니다. 물품이 없으면 중요물품 본문은 빈 목록입니다.';
    if (task === 'logSummary') return 'FULL_OUTPUT=NO. 신규·수정·정정 날짜의 완성된 [날짜-키워드] 블록만 출력합니다. 출력하지 않은 기존 날짜는 삭제 대상이 아닙니다.';
    if (task === 'personInfo') return '[[WISH_PERSON_V1]] 표식 한 쌍 사이에 현재 유효한 모든 카드를 한 줄 JSONL로 출력하고, 이번에 근거가 확인된 종료만 op=end로 넣습니다.';
    return '[TIMELINE] 안에 기존 REF와 신규 REF=NEW를 포함한 전체 카드 배열을 출력합니다. 잠금 카드는 바꾸지 않습니다.';
  }

  function manualPacketKey(room, task) { return `${String(room.chatId || '')}\u0000${task}`; }

  function manualTaskStatusHtml(room, task) {
    const checkpoint = manualCheckpointForTask(room, task);
    const draft = manualUpdateRuntime.packets.get(manualPacketKey(room, task));
    const count = manualUpdateRuntime.counts.get(String(room.chatId || ''))?.[task];
    const applied = checkpoint.baselineAt ? `시작점 ${new Date(checkpoint.baselineAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}${checkpoint.appliedAt && checkpoint.appliedAt !== checkpoint.baselineAt ? ' · 이전 수동 적용 기록 보존' : ''}` : checkpoint.appliedAt ? new Date(checkpoint.appliedAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR') : '수동 적용 기록 없음';
    const countText = count?.error ? count.error : count?.count == null ? '새 RP 범위 확인 전' : checkpoint.endMessageId ? `새 RP ${count.count}턴` : `완료된 RP ${count.count}턴 · 최초 범위`;
    const carrier = manualUpdateRuntime.carrierStatuses.get(manualPacketKey(room, task));
    const loreGuideStatus = task === 'lore' && !manualLoreGuideSlot(room).ready ? '<span class="rpcm-update-chip is-warning">자료집 지침 미연결 · 실행 잠금</span>' : '';
    const fullBuild = manualFullBuildFor(room, task);
    const completedBuild = room.manualFullBuildCompleted?.[task];
    const fullBuildStatus = fullBuild
      ? `<span class="rpcm-update-chip is-created">${task === 'lore' && !lorePacksFor(room).some(pack => lorePackManagedBy(pack) === 'ai') ? '자료집 최초 구축' : `${infoForManualTask(task)} 전체 구축`} · ${fullBuild.completedParts}구간 완료 · 다음 ${fullBuild.lastAppliedIndex + 2}턴부터 · 전체 ${fullBuild.totalTurns}턴${fullBuild.estimatedParts ? ` · 예상 약 ${fullBuild.estimatedParts}구간` : ''}</span><span class="rpcm-update-muted">각 구간 결과를 먼저 저장한 뒤 다음 구간을 생성합니다.</span>`
      : completedBuild ? `<span class="rpcm-update-chip is-created">${infoForManualTask(task)} 전체 구축 완료 · ${completedBuild.completedParts}구간 · ${completedBuild.totalTurns}턴</span>` : '';
    const carrierText = carrier?.appliedAt === checkpoint.appliedAt && carrier.roomUpdatedAt === String(room.updatedAt || '') ? carrier.text : room.pending ? '현재 주입 반영 여부 확인 필요' : '현재 주입 없음';
    const aiAreas=task==='currentItems'?['currentState','importantItems']:[task];
    const turnKeys=manualUpdateRuntime.counts.get(String(room.chatId||''))?.turnKeys||[];
    const turnLabel=key=>{const index=turnKeys.indexOf(String(key));return index>=0?`${index+1}턴`:String(key).slice(0,12);};
    const aiStatus=aiAreas.map(area=>{const row=room.aiMemoryAutomation?.areas?.[area]||{};const label=area==='currentState'?'현재상태':area==='importantItems'?'중요 물품':MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task]?.title||task;const appliedRange=row.lastAppliedStart&&row.lastAppliedEnd?`AI 적용 ${esc(turnLabel(row.lastAppliedStart))} ~ ${esc(turnLabel(row.lastAppliedEnd))}`:'AI 적용 없음';const draftRange=row.draft?` · 초안 ${Number(row.draft.turnCount)||0}턴 생성 · 검토 대기`:'';return `<span class="rpcm-update-chip ${row.draft?'is-warning':row.lastAppliedEnd?'is-created':'is-idle'}" title="AI 초안은 검토 후 적용해야 저장 결과에 반영됩니다.">${esc(label)} · ${appliedRange}${draftRange}</span>`;}).join('');
    return `${loreGuideStatus}${fullBuildStatus}<span class="rpcm-update-chip${count?.error ? ' is-warning' : ''}">${esc(countText)}</span>${aiStatus}<div class="rpcm-update-meta"><span><b>저장</b>${esc(applied)}</span>${checkpoint.appliedAt ? `<span><b>주입</b>${esc(carrierText)}</span>` : ''}</div>${draft ? `<span class="rpcm-update-chip is-created rpcm-update-waiting">${esc(({ 'result-imported':'결과 도착 · 검증 필요',reviewed:'결과 검토 중 · 다시 Diff를 확인하고 저장하세요',verifying:'저장 결과 확인 필요 · 다시 검증하세요',stale:'원문 변경 · 재검토 필요' })[room.manualPendingJobs?.[task]?.status]||'작업자료 생성 완료 · 결과 기다리는 중')} · ${esc(new Date(draft.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('ko-KR'))}</span>` : ''}`;
  }

  function infoForManualTask(task) { return MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task]?.title || '수동 업데이트'; }

  function manualOverviewRowsHtml(room) {
    const counts = manualUpdateRuntime.counts.get(String(room.chatId || ''));
    return Object.entries(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS).map(([task, info]) => {
      const count = counts?.[task];
      const label = count?.error || (count?.count == null ? '범위 확인 전' : `새 RP ${count.count}턴`);
      return `<span class="rpcm-update-overview-row" data-key="update-overview-${task}"><strong>${esc(info.title)}</strong><em class="${count?.error ? 'is-warning' : ''}">${esc(label)}</em></span>`;
    }).join('');
  }
  function renderManualUpdateOverviewHtml(room) {
    return `<section class="rpcm-update-overview" aria-label="수동 업데이트 바로가기"><div class="rpcm-update-overview-head"><div><strong>수동 업데이트</strong><span>새 RP 범위와 작업별 기준점을 확인합니다.</span></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-open-update>업데이트 센터 열기</button></div><div class="rpcm-update-overview-rows">${manualOverviewRowsHtml(room)}</div></section>`;
  }

  function maintenanceContextForRoom(room) {
    const originalChars = carrierOriginalCharsForUi(room);
    const budget = originalChars === null ? contextBudgetForPreview(room) : contextBudgetForCarrier(room, originalChars);
    let items = room.pending ? activePendingItems(room.pending) : snapshotSelectedItems(room, null, budget);
    const originalText = carrierOriginalTextForUi(room);
    if (originalText !== null) items = fitItemsToCarrierLimits(room, originalText, items);
    const stats = statsForItems(items);
    return { items, capacity:carrierCapacityForUi(room, stats.block, buildContextBlockFromItems(items)) };
  }

  function maintenanceViewForItem(item) {
    if (item.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item.group === 'log-auto') return 'logs';
    if (item.group === 'lore') return 'lore';
    if (item.group === 'item') return 'items';
    if (item.group === 'character') return 'characters';
    if (item.group === 'extra') return 'extra';
    if (item.group === 'knowledge' || item.group === 'person') return 'people';
    if (item.group === 'timeline' || item.group === 'story-timeline') return 'timeline';
    return 'memory';
  }

  function maintenanceRecommendations(room, items, capacity) {
    const immediate = [], updates = [], capacityItems = [];
    const push = (list, title, reason, view, detail = '', task = '', item = null) => list.push({ title, reason, view, detail, task, item });
    const duplicateLogs = duplicateLogDateGroups(room).length;
    const duplicateItems = duplicateImportantItemGroups(room).length;
    const conflicts = knowledgeConflicts(room).size;
    const loreBudget = loreBudgetInfoFor(room);
    const loreOmitted = Math.max(0, Number(loreBudget?.candidates || 0) - Number(loreBudget?.included || 0));
    if (duplicateLogs) push(immediate, '중복 로그', `${duplicateLogs}개 날짜 그룹의 시간선·내용 확인`, 'logs');
    if (duplicateItems) push(immediate, '중복 물품', `${duplicateItems}개 물품 그룹 확인`, 'items');
    if (conflicts) push(immediate, '인지 카드 충돌', `${conflicts}개 인지 충돌 확인`, 'people');
    const storyDue = Number(room.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount || 0) >= Number(room.storyTimelineReviewSettings?.threshold || 50);
    if (storyDue) push(immediate, '타임라인 검토 필요', `마지막 검토 이후 ${room.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount}턴`, 'timeline');
    if (loreOmitted) push(immediate, '자료집 용량 제외', `${loreOmitted}개 후보가 주입 예산에서 제외됨`, 'lore');
    if (room.pending?.reanchorRetry || (room.pending && !room.pending.verified)) push(immediate, '현재 주입 확인', room.pending.reanchorRetry ? 'carrier 이동 복구 대기' : '서버 반영 확인 필요', 'overview');
    if (Number(room._logBudgetInfo?.omitted || 0) > 0) push(immediate, '날짜로그 용량 제외', `${room._logBudgetInfo.omitted}개 선택 로그가 용량 때문에 제외됨`, 'logs');
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks((room.slots || []).find(slot => slot.id === 'logSummary')?.content || '');
    const accepted = new Set((room.acceptedUnknownLogKeys || []).map(String));
    if (blocks.some(block => (block.isUnknown && !accepted.has(String(block.key))) || (!block.isUnknown && !block.isSpecialDate && block.year == null))) push(immediate, '날짜로그 날짜 확인', '날짜 미상 또는 연도가 빠진 블록 확인', 'logs');

    const counts = manualUpdateRuntime.counts.get(String(room.chatId || ''));
    for (const [task, info] of Object.entries(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS)) {
      const count = Number(counts?.[task]?.count);
      const threshold = task === 'timeline' ? Math.max(20, Number(room.storyTimelineReviewSettings?.threshold || 50)) : 25;
      if (Number.isFinite(count) && count >= threshold) push(updates, info.title, `기준점 이후 완료 RP ${count}턴 · 갱신 검토`, 'update', `${count}턴`, task);
    }
    if (Number(room.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount || 0) >= Number(room.storyTimelineReviewSettings?.threshold || 50) && !updates.some(entry => entry.task === 'timeline')) push(updates, '연속성 타임라인', `마지막 검토 이후 ${room.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount}턴`, 'timeline', `${room.storyTimelineUnreviewedTurnCount}턴`);

    const limit = Number(room.maxChars || APP.defaultMaxChars);
    const usage = Number(capacity?.total || 0);
    const near = usage > APP.safeChars || usage > limit * .9 || (capacity?.byteKnown && Number(capacity.payloadBytes || 0) > APP.safeCarrierPayloadBytes * .9);
    if (near) {
      push(capacityItems, '컨텍스트 정리 권장', `${formatCount(usage)} / ${formatCount(limit)}자 · 주입 필요성과 내용 길이 확인`, 'overview');
      const large = [...(items || [])].filter(item => String(item.content || '').trim()).sort((a, b) => String(b.content || '').length - String(a.content || '').length).slice(0, 5);
      for (const item of large) {
        const chars = String(item.content || '').length;
        push(capacityItems, `${item.title || itemCategory(item)} · ${itemCategory(item)}`, `현재 ${formatCount(chars)}자 주입 · 필요성과 길이 확인`, maintenanceViewForItem(item), `${formatCount(chars)}자`, '', item);
      }
    }
    const oldLarge = (items || []).filter(item => item.group !== 'log-auto' && item.sourceSlotId !== 'logSummary' && !item.userLocked).map(item => {
      const slot = (room.slots || []).find(entry => String(entry.id) === String(item.slotId || item.sourceSlotId || ''));
      const updatedAt = item.updatedAt || slot?.updatedAt;
      const age = updatedAt ? Date.now() - new Date(updatedAt).getTime() : 0;
      return { item, age, chars:String(item.content || '').length, updatedAt };
    }).filter(row => Number.isFinite(row.age) && row.age > 180 * 86400000 && row.chars >= 2200).sort((a, b) => b.chars - a.chars).slice(0, 2);
    for (const row of oldLarge) if (!capacityItems.some(entry => entry.item && maintenanceItemKey(entry.item) === maintenanceItemKey(row.item))) push(capacityItems, `${row.item.title || itemCategory(row.item)} · ${itemCategory(row.item)}`, `큰 주입 항목 · 마지막 수정 ${new Date(row.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString('ko-KR')} · 주입 필요성 확인`, maintenanceViewForItem(row.item), `${formatCount(row.chars)}자`, '', row.item);
    const summary = capacityItems.filter(entry => !entry.item);
    const ranked = capacityItems.filter(entry => entry.item).sort((a, b) => String(b.item.content || '').length - String(a.item.content || '').length);
    capacityItems.splice(0, capacityItems.length, ...summary, ...ranked);
    return { immediate, updates, capacityItems };
  }

  function maintenanceItemKey(item) {
    if (item?.loreCardId) return `lore:${item.loreCardId}`;
    if (item?.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item?.group === 'log-auto') return `log:${item.sourceKey || item.slotId || ''}`;
    return `slot:${item?.slotId || ''}`;
  }

  function maintenanceCapacityInventory(report, items) {
    const existing = new Map(report.capacityItems.filter(entry => entry.item).map(entry => [maintenanceItemKey(entry.item), entry]));
    const summary = report.capacityItems.filter(entry => !entry.item);
    const seen = new Set();
    const rows = [...(items || [])].filter(item => {
      const key = maintenanceItemKey(item);
      if (!String(item.content || '').trim() || seen.has(key)) return false;
      seen.add(key); return true;
    })
      .sort((a, b) => String(b.content || '').length - String(a.content || '').length)
      .map(item => existing.get(maintenanceItemKey(item)) || {
        title:`${item.title || itemCategory(item)} · ${itemCategory(item)}`,
        reason:'현재 주입 중 · 내용과 주입 필요성을 직접 확인',
        view:maintenanceViewForItem(item),
        detail:`${formatCount(String(item.content || '').length)}자`,
        task:'', item,
      });
    return [...summary, ...rows];
  }

  function refreshMaintenanceOverview(overlay, room) {
    const count = overlay?.querySelector('[data-maintenance-count]');
    if (!count || !room || state.currentRoom !== room) return;
    const { items, capacity } = maintenanceContextForRoom(room);
    const report = maintenanceRecommendations(room, items, capacity);
    count.textContent = `정리 추천 ${report.updates.length + report.capacityItems.length}건`;
  }

  function openMaintenanceTarget(overlay, entry) {
    const view = entry.task ? 'update' : entry.view;
    showWorkView(overlay, view, true);
    const item = entry.item;
    if (view === 'lore' && item?.loreCardId && ![...overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-lore-card]')].some(node => node.dataset.loreCard === String(item.loreCardId))) {
      Object.assign(loreUiFor(state.currentRoom), { search:'', type:'', mode:'', pack:'' });
      const list = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-list');
      if (list) rpcmPatchHtml(list, renderLoreListHtml(state.currentRoom));
      for (const id of ['rpcm-lore-search','rpcm-lore-filter-pack','rpcm-lore-filter-type','rpcm-lore-filter-mode']) {
        const field = overlay.querySelector('#' + id);
        if (field) field.value = '';
      }
    }
    const target = entry.task ? overlay.querySelector(`[data-update-task="${entry.task}"]`)
      : item?.loreCardId ? [...overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-lore-card]')].find(node => node.dataset.loreCard === String(item.loreCardId))
        : item?.slotId ? [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]')].find(node => node.dataset.slotId === String(item.slotId)) : null;
    if (target?.tagName === 'DETAILS') target.open = true;
    for (let parent = target?.parentElement; parent && parent !== overlay; parent = parent.parentElement) if (parent.tagName === 'DETAILS') parent.open = true;
    const pack = target?.closest('.rpcm-lore-pack');
    if (pack) pack.open = true;
    target?.scrollIntoView({ block:'center' });
    target?.querySelector('h3')?.focus?.({ preventScroll:true });
    return target;
  }

  function maintenanceActionsForEntry(room, entry) {
    if (entry.task) return [{ code:'update', label:'작업자료 만들기' }];
    const item = entry.item;
    if (!item) return [];
    if (item.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item.group === 'log-auto') {
      const key = String(item.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');
      return [
        ...(key && (room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String).includes(key) ? [{ code:'log-unpin', label:'📌 고정 해제' }] : []),
        ...(key && (room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String).includes(key) ? [{ code:'log-unselect', label:'직접 선택 해제' }] : []),
      ];
    }
    if (item.group === 'lore') {
      const pack = lorePacksFor(room).find(pack => String(pack.id) === String(item.lorePackId));
      const card = (pack?.cards || []).find(card => String(card.id) === String(item.loreCardId));
      if (!card) return [];
      return [
        ...(card.mode === 'always' ? [{ code:'lore-auto', label:'항상 → 자동' }] : []),
        ...(card.mode === 'manual' && card.manualSelected !== false ? [{ code:'lore-unselect', label:'직접 선택 해제' }] : []),
        ...(card.mode !== 'off' ? [{ code:'lore-off', label:'주입 끄기' }] : []),
        { code:'edit', label:'편집' },
      ];
    }
    if (item.slotId === 'currentState') return [{ code:'update-current', label:'현재상태 업데이트' }, { code:'edit', label:'원문 편집' }];
    if (item.group === 'timeline' || item.group === 'story-timeline') return [{ code:'update-timeline', label:'타임라인 업데이트' }];
    if (item.group === 'knowledge' || item.group === 'person') return [{ code:'update-person', label:'인물정보 업데이트' }];
    const slot = (room.slots || []).find(slot => String(slot.id) === String(item.slotId));
    if (!slot || slot.id === 'logSummary') return [];
    return [
      ...(slot.autoPinned ? [{ code:'slot-unpin', label:'📌 고정 해제' }] : []),
      ...((slot.group === 'character' || slot.group === 'item') && !slot.autoExcluded ? [{ code:'slot-exclude', label:'자동 선택 제외' }] : []),
      ...(slot.enabled && !slot.autoPinned ? [{ code:'slot-off', label:'주입 끄기' }] : []),
      { code:'edit', label:'편집' },
    ];
  }

  async function applyMaintenanceAction(room, entry, code) {
    if (state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 방이 바뀌었습니다.');
    const roomKey = String(room.chatId || '');
    if (state.autoSaveTimers.has(roomKey) || state.saveStatus === 'saving' || state.saveStatus === 'error') throw new Error('기존 로컬 저장이 끝난 뒤 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    if (!maintenanceActionsForEntry(room, entry).some(action => action.code === code)) throw new Error('이 항목에 사용할 수 없는 작업입니다.');
    const item = entry.item;
    if (code === 'slot-off') {
      const slot = (room.slots || []).find(slot => String(slot.id) === String(item.slotId));
      await setSlotEnabledDuringPending(room, slot, false);
      return;
    }
    if (code === 'slot-unpin' || code === 'slot-exclude') {
      const slot = (room.slots || []).find(slot => String(slot.id) === String(item.slotId));
      const previous = { autoPinned:slot.autoPinned, autoExcluded:slot.autoExcluded, lastAutoMatch:slot.lastAutoMatch };
      if (code === 'slot-unpin') {
        slot.autoPinned = false;
        if (slot.lastAutoMatch === '사용자 고정') slot.lastAutoMatch = '';
      } else {
        slot.autoExcluded = true; slot.autoPinned = false;
        if (slot.lastAutoMatch === '사용자 고정') slot.lastAutoMatch = '';
      }
      try { await saveRoom(room); }
      catch (error) { Object.assign(slot, previous); throw error; }
      return;
    }
    if (code.startsWith('lore-')) {
      const pack = lorePacksFor(room).find(pack => String(pack.id) === String(item.lorePackId));
      const card = (pack?.cards || []).find(card => String(card.id) === String(item.loreCardId));
      await commitLoreMutation(room, () => {
        if (code === 'lore-auto') card.mode = 'auto';
        else if (code === 'lore-unselect') card.manualSelected = false;
        else card.mode = 'off';
      });
      return;
    }
    if (code === 'log-unpin' || code === 'log-unselect') {
      const key = String(item.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');
      const field = code === 'log-unpin' ? 'autoLogPinnedKeys' : 'manualLogSelectedKeys';
      const before = [...(room[field] || [])], oldPending = room.pending ? clonePendingItems(room.pending.items) : null;
      room[field] = before.map(String).filter(value => value !== key);
      try { if (room.pending) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'maintenance-log-selection'); else await saveRoom(room); }
      catch (error) {
        room[field] = before;
        if (room.pending && oldPending) room.pending.items = oldPending;
        await saveRoom(room).catch(() => {});
        throw error;
      }
    }
  }

  function openMaintenanceDialog(room) {
    const overlay = state.modal;
    if (!overlay || state.currentRoom !== room) return;
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-maintenance-backdrop')?.remove();
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
    backdrop.id = 'rpcm-maintenance-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML = `<section class="rpcm-maintenance-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="정리 점검"><header><div><h2>정리 점검</h2><p>현재 주입과 저장 설정을 로컬에서 확인합니다. 열기만 해서는 변경되지 않습니다.</p></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-maintenance-ai="diet">AI 필요성 검토</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-maintenance-ai="update">AI 갱신 우선순위</button><button type="button" data-maintenance-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button></header><div class="rpcm-maintenance-capacity" aria-live="polite"></div><nav class="rpcm-maintenance-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="정리 점검 종류"></nav><div class="rpcm-maintenance-body" id="rpcm-maintenance-panel" role="tabpanel"></div><footer><div class="rpcm-maintenance-summary" aria-live="polite"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-maintenance-bulk disabled>선택 항목 주입 끄기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-maintenance-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="text">닫기</button></footer></section>`;
    const selected = new Set(), expanded = new Set();
    let currentEntries = [], currentCapacityEntries = [], currentCapacity = null;
    let activeTab = '', capacityFilter = 'all', showAll = false, busy = false, resultMessage = '';
    const close = () => { if (!busy) backdrop.remove(); };
    const filterCapacity = (entry) => {
      const item = entry.item;
      if (!item || capacityFilter === 'all') return true;
      const actions = maintenanceActionsForEntry(room, entry);
      const reason = String(item.recallReason || item.loreBaseReason || '');
      const slot = (room.slots || []).find(slot => String(slot.id) === String(item.slotId || ''));
      const logKey = String(item.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');
      const directlyChosen = !!slot?.autoPinned || (item.group === 'lore' && ['manual','always'].includes(item.loreMode)) || (item.group === 'log-auto' && ((room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String).includes(logKey) || (room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String).includes(logKey)));
      if (capacityFilter === 'off') return actions.some(action => ['slot-off','lore-off'].includes(action.code));
      if (capacityFilter === 'core') return item.slotId === 'currentState' || ['timeline','story-timeline','knowledge','person'].includes(item.group);
      if (capacityFilter === 'auto') return !directlyChosen && (item.group === 'log-auto' || /자동|감지|관련|최근/.test(reason) || (item.group === 'lore' && item.loreMode === 'auto'));
      if (capacityFilter === 'manual') return directlyChosen || /직접|고정/.test(reason);
      return true;
    };
    const render = (resetScroll = false) => {
      const { items, capacity } = maintenanceContextForRoom(room);
      currentCapacity = capacity;
      const report = maintenanceRecommendations(room, items, capacity);
      currentCapacityEntries = maintenanceCapacityInventory(report, items);
      const capacityRows = currentCapacityEntries.filter(entry => entry.item);
      const capacityNotes = currentCapacityEntries.filter(entry => !entry.item);
      if (!activeTab) activeTab = report.immediate.length ? 'immediate' : report.updates.length ? 'updates' : 'capacity';
      currentEntries = [];
      const eligible = new Set(capacityRows.filter(entry => maintenanceActionsForEntry(room, entry).some(action => ['slot-off','lore-off'].includes(action.code))).map(entry => maintenanceItemKey(entry.item)));
      for (const key of selected) if (!eligible.has(key)) selected.delete(key);
      const selectedEntries = capacityRows.filter(entry => selected.has(maintenanceItemKey(entry.item)));
