    backdrop.innerHTML=`<section class="rpcm-update-dialog rpcm-update-result-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${esc(title)}"><header><div><h2>${esc(title)}</h2><p>${esc(description)}</p></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-update-dialog-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button></header><div class="rpcm-update-result-body"><div class="rpcm-update-import-grid"><label>직접 붙여넣기<textarea data-direct-input spellcheck="false" placeholder="${esc(placeholder)}"></textarea></label></div><div class="rpcm-update-result-status" role="status">붙여넣은 내용은 검증 후에만 저장됩니다. 수동 업데이트 체크포인트는 이동하지 않습니다.</div><div class="rpcm-update-diff" hidden></div></div><footer><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-direct-current>현재 저장본 불러오기</button><span>복구용 직접 반영 · 체크포인트 유지</span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-update-dialog-close">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-direct-preview>검증 · Diff</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-direct-apply disabled>확인하고 저장</button></footer></section>`;
    const ta=backdrop.querySelector('[data-direct-input]'), status=backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-update-result-status'), diff=backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-update-diff'), previewBtn=backdrop.querySelector('[data-direct-preview]'), applyBtn=backdrop.querySelector('[data-direct-apply]');
    let prepared=null,saving=false;
    const invalidate=()=>{prepared=null;applyBtn.disabled=true;diff.hidden=true;diff.innerHTML='';status.textContent='입력 변경됨 · 다시 검증해 주세요.';};
    ta.addEventListener('input',invalidate);
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-direct-current]').onclick=()=>{ta.value=currentText();invalidate();status.textContent='현재 저장본을 불러왔습니다. 수정하거나 새 결과로 바꾼 뒤 검증해 주세요.';ta.focus();};
    previewBtn.onclick=()=>{previewBtn.disabled=true;applyBtn.disabled=true;status.textContent='형식과 변경 내용을 확인하는 중…';try{prepared=onPrepare(ta.value);const before=prepared.before||'',after=prepared.after||'';const side=buildAiSideBySideDiff(before,after);const rows=side.rows.filter(row=>row.type!=='same').slice(0,220);const changed=side.stats.changed+side.stats.added+side.stats.removed;diff.innerHTML=`<p class="rpcm-update-diff-summary">${esc(prepared.summary||'미리보기')}</p><details class="rpcm-update-diff-part" open><summary>기존/적용 후 비교 · 변경 ${changed}줄</summary><div class="rpcm-update-diff-columns"><div><strong>기존 저장본</strong><pre>${esc(before||'(비어 있음)')}</pre></div><div><strong>적용 후</strong><pre>${esc(after||'(비어 있음)')}</pre></div></div>${rows.length?`<details><summary>변경 줄만 보기</summary><div class="rpcm-update-diff-lines">${rows.map(row=>`<div class="${row.type}"><del>${esc(row.old)}</del><ins>${esc(row.next)}</ins></div>`).join('')}</div></details>`:''}</details>`;diff.hidden=false;applyBtn.disabled=false;status.textContent='검증 완료 · Diff를 확인한 뒤 저장하세요.';}catch(error){prepared=null;status.textContent=`적용 불가: ${error.message}`;}finally{previewBtn.disabled=false;}};
    applyBtn.onclick=async()=>{if(!prepared||saving)return;if(!confirm(`${title}\n\n${prepared.summary||''}\n\n체크포인트는 이동하지 않습니다. 계속할까요?`))return;saving=true;applyBtn.disabled=true;previewBtn.disabled=true;status.textContent='저장 중…';try{const message=await onApply(prepared);status.textContent=`완료 · ${message}`;notify(`${title} 완료 · ${message}`,message.includes('실패')?'warn':'success',6500);renderModalIfOpen();}catch(error){status.textContent=`저장 실패: ${error.message}`;notify(`${title} 실패: ${error.message}`,'error',7000);applyBtn.disabled=false;previewBtn.disabled=false;}finally{saving=false;}};
    const close=()=>{if(!saving)backdrop.remove();};backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-update-dialog-close').forEach(button=>button.onclick=close);backdrop.onclick=event=>{if(event.target===backdrop)close();};backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')close();};document.body.appendChild(backdrop);ta.focus();
  }

  function prepareDirectItemsReplace(room, value) {
    const raw=stripAiOutputWrapper(String(value||'').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'').trim());
    if(!raw||!raw.startsWith('- **'))throw new Error('중요물품.txt 전체본을 붙여넣어 주세요. 첫 줄은 “- **”여야 합니다.');
    const parsed=parseImportantItemBulkText(raw), headings=raw.match(/^[-*]\s+\*\*.+?\*\*/gm)||[];
    if(!parsed.length||parsed.length!==headings.length)throw new Error('일부 물품을 읽지 못했습니다. 상위 물품 제목과 본문 형식을 확인해 주세요.');
    const key=t=>cleanImportantItemTitle(t).replace(/\s*[｜|]\s*/g,'|').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
    if(new Set(parsed.map(item=>key(item.title))).size!==parsed.length)throw new Error('붙여넣은 목록에 중복 제목이 있습니다.');
    const candidate=structuredClone(room),old=(candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item'),by=new Map();
    for(const slot of old){const k=key(slot.title);if(by.has(k))throw new Error('기존 중요 물품에 같은 제목이 둘 이상 있습니다. 중복을 먼저 정리해 주세요.');by.set(k,slot);}
    const next=parsed.map(item=>{const prev=by.get(key(item.title));return prev?{...prev,title:item.title,content:item.content}:{...makeDynamicSlot('item',item.title),content:item.content};});
    candidate.slots=(candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group!=='item').concat(next);
    if(candidate.pending){const slots=new Map(candidate.slots.map(slot=>[String(slot.id),slot])),oldIds=new Set(old.map(slot=>String(slot.id)));const refresh=items=>(items||[]).map(item=>{if(!oldIds.has(String(item.slotId||'')))return item;const slot=slots.get(String(item.slotId||''));return slot?{...item,title:slot.title,content:String(slot.content||'').trim()}:null;}).filter(Boolean);candidate.pending.items=refresh(candidate.pending.items);candidate.pending.quickRemovedItems=refresh(candidate.pending.quickRemovedItems);}
    return{candidate,baseSnapshot:JSON.stringify(room),before:allImportantItemRawText(room),after:raw,summary:`중요 물품 전체 교체 · ${old.length}개 → ${next.length}개`};
  }

  async function saveDirectPasteCandidate(room,prepared,payload,task){
    if(JSON.stringify(room)!==prepared.baseSnapshot)throw new Error('미리보기 후 방 데이터가 바뀌었습니다. 변경 내용을 다시 검토해 주세요.');
    const expected=await longTermHash(payload(prepared.candidate));
    if(task)await stampVerifiedResult(prepared.candidate,task,'direct-paste');
    await saveRoom(prepared.candidate);
    const stored=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
    if(!stored||await longTermHash(payload(stored))!==expected)
      throw new Error('저장 후 IndexedDB 결과 확인에 실패했습니다. 적용 성공으로 표시하지 않습니다.');
    if(JSON.stringify(room)!==prepared.baseSnapshot)
      throw new Error('저장 중 화면 데이터가 바뀌었습니다. 새로고침 후 저장본을 확인해 주세요.');
    const verified=task?await finalizeStoredResultReceipts(room,stored,[task],'direct-paste'):stored;
    rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,verified);
  }

  async function applyDirectItemsReplace(room, prepared) {
    await saveDirectPasteCandidate(room,prepared,data=>JSON.stringify((data.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='item')),'currentItems');
    if(room.pending){try{const result=await syncPendingCarrier(room,'direct-items-replace');return result?.suspended||room.pending?.reanchorRetry?'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기':'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입도 최신 물품으로 갱신';}catch(error){return`로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 갱신 실패: ${error.message}`;}}
    return'로컬 저장 완료 · 다음 주입부터 최신 물품 사용';
  }

  function prepareDirectTimelineReplace(room, value) {
    let raw=stripAiOutputWrapper(String(value||'').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'').trim());const audit=raw.match(/^\[TIMELINE_AUDIT\][\s\S]*?\[\/TIMELINE_AUDIT\]/i);if(audit)raw=raw.slice(audit[0].length).trim();
    if(!/\[TIMELINE\]/i.test(raw)||!/\[\/TIMELINE\]/i.test(raw))throw new Error('[TIMELINE] 시작·종료가 포함된 전체 결과가 필요합니다.');
    const candidate=structuredClone(room),run=createStoryTimelineRun(candidate,activeLogTimelineLabel(candidate),'direct-replace','FULL');
    const beforeCards=snapshotStoryTimelineCardsForLabel(candidate,run.timelineLabel),preview=parseStoryTimelineImport(raw,candidate,{run});
    if(!preview?.cards?.length)throw new Error('타임라인 카드를 읽지 못했습니다.');backupStoryTimeline(candidate,'직접 원문 붙여넣기 전');applyStoryTimelinePreviewForRun(candidate,run,preview);candidate.storyTimelineLastDiff=preview.diff||[];candidate.updatedAt=nowIso();
    return{candidate,baseSnapshot:JSON.stringify(room),before:serializeStoryTimelineRefMaster(beforeCards,structuredClone(run),true),after:serializeStoryTimelineRefMaster(snapshotStoryTimelineCardsForLabel(candidate,run.timelineLabel),run,true),summary:`${run.timelineLabel} · 카드 ${beforeCards.length}개 → ${preview.cards.length}개 · 변경 ${(preview.diff||[]).length}개`};
  }

  async function applyDirectTimelineReplace(room, prepared) {
    await saveDirectPasteCandidate(room,prepared,data=>JSON.stringify(data.storyTimelineCards||[]),'timeline');
    if(room.pending){try{const result=await rebuildPendingStoryTimelineItem(room,'direct-timeline-replace');return result?.suspended||room.pending?.reanchorRetry?'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기':result?.suppressed?'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입의 타임라인 OFF 상태 유지':'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입도 최신 타임라인으로 갱신';}catch(error){return`로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 갱신 실패: ${error.message}`;}}
    return'로컬 저장 완료 · 다음 주입부터 최신 타임라인 사용';
  }

  function prepareDirectPortableLoreReplace(room,data) {
    if(data.version!==2||!Array.isArray(data.packs)||data.packs.length>200)throw new Error('portable 자료집 version 또는 팩 목록이 올바르지 않습니다.');
    const candidate=structuredClone(room),old=lorePacksFor(candidate),used=new Set(),next=[],now=nowIso();
    if(new Set(data.packs.map(pack=>String(pack?.kind)+'|'+String(pack?.title||'').trim())).size!==data.packs.length)throw new Error('portable 자료팩 제목·종류가 중복됩니다.');
    let protectedCount=0;
    for(const [index,raw] of data.packs.entries()){
      if(!raw||typeof raw!=='object'||!String(raw.title||'').trim()||String(raw.title).length>160||!['room','reference'].includes(raw.kind)||!Array.isArray(raw.cards)||raw.cards.length>2000)throw new Error(`${index+1}번째 자료팩 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.`);
      const matches=old.filter(pack=>String(pack.id)===String(raw.id||'')||pack.kind===raw.kind&&String(pack.title||'').trim()===String(raw.title).trim());
      if(matches.length>1)throw new Error('같은 자료팩 ID/제목이 여러 개입니다. 충돌을 정리한 뒤 가져오세요.');
      const existing=matches[0];
      if(existing&&used.has(existing.id))throw new Error('portable 자료팩이 중복됩니다.');
      if(existing)used.add(existing.id);
      const normalized=raw.cards.map((card,i)=>loreImportNormalizeCard(card,`${raw.title} · ${i+1}번째 카드`));
      if(new Set(normalized.map(card=>card.type+'|'+card.title)).size!==normalized.length)throw new Error('portable 팩에 같은 제목·유형 카드가 중복됩니다.');
      if(existing&&lorePackManagedBy(existing)!=='ai'){next.push(existing);protectedCount+=existing.cards?.length||0;continue;}
      const pack=existing?{...existing,title:String(raw.title).trim(),kind:raw.kind,cards:[],updatedAt:now}:{id:makeLoreId('pack'),title:String(raw.title).trim(),kind:raw.kind,enabled:true,managedBy:'ai',cards:[],createdAt:now,updatedAt:now};
      const seenCards=new Set();
      for(const [i,fields] of normalized.entries()){
        const rawCard=raw.cards[i],matches=(existing?.cards||[]).filter(card=>String(card.id)===String(rawCard.id||'')||card.title===fields.title&&card.type===fields.type);
        if(matches.length>1)throw new Error('카드 ID/제목이 충돌합니다.');
        const prior=matches[0];if(prior&&seenCards.has(prior.id))throw new Error('카드가 중복됩니다.');
        if(prior)seenCards.add(prior.id);
        if(prior&&loreCardUserLocked(prior,existing)){pack.cards.push(prior);protectedCount++;}
        else pack.cards.push(prior?{...prior,...fields,updatedAt:now}:{id:makeLoreId('card'),...fields,...LORE_NEW_CARD_DEFAULTS,managedBy:'ai',userLocked:false,createdAt:now,updatedAt:now});
      }
      for(const card of existing?.cards||[])if(!seenCards.has(card.id)&&loreCardUserLocked(card,existing)){pack.cards.push(card);protectedCount++;}
      next.push(pack);
    }
    for(const pack of old)if(!used.has(pack.id)){
      if(lorePackManagedBy(pack)!=='ai'){next.push(pack);protectedCount+=pack.cards?.length||0;}
      else{const cards=(pack.cards||[]).filter(card=>loreCardUserLocked(card,pack));if(cards.length){next.push({...pack,cards});protectedCount+=cards.length;}}
    }
    candidate.loreBackups=[{at:now,reason:'portable 전체 교체 전',packs:structuredClone(old)},...(candidate.loreBackups||[])].slice(0,10);
    candidate.lorePacks=next;candidate.updatedAt=now;
    return {candidate,baseSnapshot:JSON.stringify(room),before:JSON.stringify(lorePortableExport(room),null,2),after:JSON.stringify(lorePortableExport(candidate),null,2),summary:`portable 전체 교체 · 팩 ${old.length}→${next.length} · 카드 ${old.reduce((n,p)=>n+(p.cards||[]).length,0)}→${next.reduce((n,p)=>n+p.cards.length,0)} · 사용자 관리/잠금 ${protectedCount}개 유지 · 주입 설정 유지`};
  }

  function prepareDirectLorePaste(room, value) {
    const raw=String(value||'').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'').trim();if(!raw)throw new Error('WISH_LORE_V2 JSON을 붙여넣어 주세요.');if(raw.length>600000)throw new Error('JSON이 안전 한도를 넘습니다.');if(raw.startsWith('{')){let data;try{data=JSON.parse(raw);}catch(_){throw new Error('자료집 JSON을 읽을 수 없습니다.');}if(data?.format==='wish-rp-manager-lore-portable')return prepareDirectPortableLoreReplace(room,data);}const parsed=parseManualLoreResult(raw),candidate=structuredClone(room),refs=manualLoreRefs(candidate),packByRef=new Map(),cardByRef=new Map();
    for(const[ref,id]of Object.entries(refs.packIds)){const pack=lorePacksFor(candidate).find(item=>String(item.id)===id);if(pack)packByRef.set(ref,pack);}for(const[ref,identity]of Object.entries(refs.cardIds)){const pack=lorePacksFor(candidate).find(item=>String(item.id)===identity.packId),card=(pack?.cards||[]).find(item=>String(item.id)===identity.cardId);if(pack&&card)cardByRef.set(ref,{pack,card});}
    const newPackRefs=new Set(parsed.operations.filter(op=>op.op==='pack_upsert'&&op.ref.startsWith('NEW_')).map(op=>op.ref));for(const op of parsed.operations){if(op.op==='pack_upsert'&&!op.ref.startsWith('NEW_')&&!packByRef.has(op.ref))throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 현재 자료집 REF에 없는 팩입니다.`);if(op.op==='card_upsert'&&!op.ref.startsWith('NEW_')&&!cardByRef.has(op.ref))throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 현재 자료집 REF에 없는 카드입니다.`);if(op.op==='card_upsert'&&!packByRef.has(op.packRef)&&!newPackRefs.has(op.packRef))throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 대상 팩 ${op.packRef}를 찾지 못했습니다.`);}
    let added=0,changed=0,protectedCount=0,moved=0,unchanged=0;
    for(const op of parsed.operations.filter(op=>op.op==='pack_upsert')){const old=packByRef.get(op.ref),after={title:String(op.title||'').trim(),kind:op.kind==='room'?'room':'reference'};if(!old){if(lorePacksFor(candidate).some(pack=>pack.kind===after.kind&&String(pack.title||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')===after.title.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')))throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 같은 제목·종류의 팩이 이미 있습니다.`);const pack={id:makeLoreId('pack'),...after,enabled:true,managedBy:'ai',cards:[],createdAt:nowIso(),updatedAt:nowIso()};candidate.lorePacks.push(pack);packByRef.set(op.ref,pack);added++;}else if(old.title===after.title&&old.kind===after.kind)unchanged++;else if(lorePackManagedBy(old)!=='ai')protectedCount++;else{Object.assign(old,after,{updatedAt:nowIso()});changed++;}}
    for(const op of parsed.operations.filter(op=>op.op==='card_upsert')){const target=packByRef.get(op.packRef),old=cardByRef.get(op.ref),fields=loreCardContentFields(op),sourceTurnKey=String(op.sourceTurnKey||old?.card.sourceTurnKey||''),evidence=String(op.evidence||old?.card.evidence||'');if(old&&JSON.stringify(loreCardContentFields(old.card))===JSON.stringify(fields)&&old.pack===target){unchanged++;continue;}if(lorePackManagedBy(target)!=='ai'||(old&&loreCardUserLocked(old.card,old.pack))){protectedCount++;continue;}if((target.cards||[]).some(card=>card!==old?.card&&manualLoreDuplicateKey(card)===manualLoreDuplicateKey(fields)))throw new Error(`${op.ref}: 같은 제목·유형 카드가 이미 있습니다.`);if(!old){target.cards.push({id:makeLoreId('card'),...fields,sourceTurnKey,evidence,...LORE_NEW_CARD_DEFAULTS,managedBy:'ai',userLocked:false,createdAt:nowIso(),updatedAt:nowIso()});target.updatedAt=nowIso();added++;}else{if(old.pack!==target){old.pack.cards=old.pack.cards.filter(card=>card!==old.card);target.cards.push(old.card);old.pack.updatedAt=nowIso();moved++;}Object.assign(old.card,fields,{sourceTurnKey,evidence,updatedAt:nowIso()});target.updatedAt=nowIso();changed++;}}
    candidate.updatedAt=nowIso();return{candidate,baseSnapshot:JSON.stringify(room),before:JSON.stringify(lorePortableExport(room),null,2),after:JSON.stringify(lorePortableExport(candidate),null,2),summary:`자료집 증분 반영 · 추가 ${added} · 수정 ${changed} · 변경 없음 ${unchanged} · 보호 제외 ${protectedCount} · 팩 이동 ${moved} · 삭제 0`};
  }

  async function applyDirectLorePaste(room, prepared) {
    await saveDirectPasteCandidate(room,prepared,data=>JSON.stringify(data.lorePacks||[]),'lore');
    if(room.pending){
      try{
        const result=await aiMemorySyncPending(room,'lore');
        return result?.suspended||room.pending?.reanchorRetry?'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기':'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 최신 자료집 반영';
      }catch(error){return `로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 실패: ${error.message}`;}
    }
    return '로컬 저장 완료 · 다음 주입부터 최신 자료집 사용';
  }

  function openManualRpExportDialog(room) {
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop')?.remove();
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
    backdrop.id = 'rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML = `<section class="rpcm-update-dialog rpcm-update-packet-choice" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="RP 원문 내보내기"><header><div><h2>RP 원문 내보내기</h2><p>완료된 USER / AI 턴을 UTF-8 TXT로 저장합니다. 지침과 업데이트 결과는 포함하지 않습니다.</p></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-update-dialog-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button></header><div class="rpcm-update-range-controls"><label>내보낼 범위 <select data-export-range><option value="full">전체 RP</option><option value="custom">범위 지정</option></select></label><div data-export-custom hidden><label>시작 턴 <select data-export-start></select></label><label>종료 턴 <select data-export-end></select></label></div></div><div class="rpcm-update-packet-status" role="status">완료된 RP 턴을 불러오는 중…</div><footer><span>원문 다운로드는 저장 데이터와 체크포인트를 바꾸지 않습니다.</span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-update-dialog-close">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-export-download disabled>TXT 다운로드</button></footer></section>`;
    let turns = null, closed = false;
    const range = backdrop.querySelector('[data-export-range]');
    const start = backdrop.querySelector('[data-export-start]');
    const end = backdrop.querySelector('[data-export-end]');
    const status = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-update-packet-status');
    const button = backdrop.querySelector('[data-export-download]');
    const close = () => { closed = true; backdrop.remove(); };
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-update-dialog-close').forEach(item => item.addEventListener('click', close));
    backdrop.addEventListener('click', event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(); });
    backdrop.addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') close(); });
    range.addEventListener('change', () => { backdrop.querySelector('[data-export-custom]').hidden = range.value !== 'custom'; });
    button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (!turns) return;
      const first = range.value === 'custom' ? turns.findIndex(turn => String(turn.key) === start.value) : 0;
      const last = range.value === 'custom' ? turns.findIndex(turn => String(turn.key) === end.value) : turns.length - 1;
      if (first < 0 || last < first) { status.textContent = '시작·종료 RP 순서를 확인해 주세요.'; return; }
      const selected = turns.slice(first, last + 1);
      downloadText(`\uFEFF${formatAiDialogueTurns(selected)}\n`, `RP_원문_${range.value === 'full' ? '전체' : `${first + 1}-${last + 1}`}_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.txt`, 'text/plain');
      status.textContent = `${selected.length}개 완료 RP 턴을 다운로드했습니다. 체크포인트는 변경되지 않았습니다.`;
    });
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    void (async () => {
      try {
        turns = await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
        if (closed || !backdrop.isConnected) return;
        if (!turns.length) throw new Error('완료된 RP 턴이 없습니다.');
        const options = turns.map((turn, index) => `<option value="${esc(String(turn.key))}">${esc(manualTurnOption(turn, index))}</option>`).join('');
        start.innerHTML = options; end.innerHTML = options;
        start.selectedIndex = 0; end.selectedIndex = turns.length - 1;
        button.disabled = false;
        status.textContent = `${turns.length}개 완료 RP 턴을 확인했습니다. 전체 기록 조회 한도에 걸리지 않았습니다.`;
      } catch (error) { if (!closed) status.textContent = `내보내기 불가: ${error.message}`; }
    })();
  }

  const manualMoreMenuRuntime={close:null,menu:null};
  function bindManualMoreMenus(overlay){
    if(manualMoreMenuRuntime.menu&&!manualMoreMenuRuntime.menu.isConnected)manualMoreMenuRuntime.close?.();
    const menus=[...overlay.querySelectorAll(':is(#rpcm-section-update,#rpcm-section-lore,#rpcm-section-compression) .rpcm-update-more')];
    for(const menu of menus){
      if(menu._rpcmMoreBound)continue;menu._rpcmMoreBound=true;
      menu.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{
        if(!menu.open){if(manualMoreMenuRuntime.menu===menu)manualMoreMenuRuntime.close?.();return;}
        manualMoreMenuRuntime.close?.();
        if(!menu.isConnected){menu.open=false;return;}
        overlay.querySelectorAll(':is(#rpcm-section-update,#rpcm-section-lore,#rpcm-section-compression) .rpcm-update-more').forEach(other=>{if(other!==menu)other.open=false;});
        const summary=menu.querySelector('summary'),panel=menu.querySelector('.rpcm-update-more-actions');
        const anchor=summary.getBoundingClientRect();
        panel.style.visibility='hidden';panel.style.left='8px';panel.style.top='8px';
        const rect=panel.getBoundingClientRect(),bounds=overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-modal')?.getBoundingClientRect()||overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
        // A transformed modal makes fixed descendants use its coordinate origin.
        const originX=rect.left-8,originY=rect.top-8;
        const right=Math.min(innerWidth-8,bounds.right-8),leftBound=Math.max(8,bounds.left+8);
        const bottom=Math.min(innerHeight-8,bounds.bottom-8),top=Math.max(8,bounds.top+8);
        panel.style.maxHeight=Math.max(40,bottom-top)+'px';
        const height=panel.getBoundingClientRect().height;
        const left=Math.max(leftBound,Math.min(anchor.right-rect.width,right-rect.width));
        const below=anchor.bottom+6,above=anchor.top-height-6;
        const y=Math.max(top,Math.min(below+height<=bottom?below:above,bottom-height));
        panel.style.left=(left-originX)+'px';panel.style.top=(y-originY)+'px';panel.style.visibility='visible';
        const close=()=>{panel.style.visibility='hidden';menu.open=false;document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',outside,true);document.removeEventListener('keydown',keyboard,true);window.removeEventListener('resize',close);overlay.removeEventListener('scroll',scroll,true);if(manualMoreMenuRuntime.close===close){manualMoreMenuRuntime.close=null;manualMoreMenuRuntime.menu=null;}};
        const outside=event=>{if(!menu.isConnected||!menu.contains(event.target))close();};
        const keyboard=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();close();summary.focus({preventScroll:true});}};
        const scroll=event=>{if(!panel.contains(event.target))close();};
        manualMoreMenuRuntime.close=close;manualMoreMenuRuntime.menu=menu;
        document.addEventListener('pointerdown',outside,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',keyboard,true);window.addEventListener('resize',close);overlay.addEventListener('scroll',scroll,true);
      });
      menu.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(manualMoreMenuRuntime.menu===menu&&event.target.closest('.rpcm-update-more-actions button'))manualMoreMenuRuntime.close?.();});
    }
  }

  function bindManualUpdateCenter(overlay, room) {
    bindIntegratedCenter(overlay,room);
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-update-batch')?.addEventListener('click',()=>openManualBatchDialog(room));
    bindManualMoreMenus(overlay);
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-update-export')?.addEventListener('click', () => openManualRpExportDialog(room));
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-manual-recovery')?.addEventListener('click', () => openManualRecoveryDialog(room));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-full-next]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      button.disabled = true;
      const task = button.dataset.fullNext;
      try {
        const progress = manualFullBuildFor(room, task);
        if (!progress) throw new Error('이어갈 전체 구축 상태가 없습니다.');
        const result = await makeManualFullBuildPart(room, task, progress.packetMode, false);
        showManualFullBuildPacket(room, task, result);
      } catch (error) { notify(`전체 구축 중단: ${error.message}`, 'warn', 7000); if (button.isConnected) button.disabled = false; }
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-full-cancel]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      button.disabled = true;
      try { await cancelManualFullBuild(room, button.dataset.fullCancel); renderModalIfOpen(); notify('전체 구축 진행 상태를 종료했습니다. 이미 저장한 결과는 유지됩니다.', 'success'); }
      catch (error) { notify(`전체 구축 취소 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error'); if (button.isConnected) button.disabled = false; }
    }));
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-update-refresh')?.addEventListener('click', () => { void refreshManualUpdateCounts(overlay, room, true); });
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-manual-integrity-check')?.addEventListener('click', async event => {
      const button=event.currentTarget;button.disabled=true;button.textContent='저장본 확인 중…';
      try{
        await openManualIntegrityDialog(room);
      }catch(error){notify(`적용 누락 점검 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
      finally{button.disabled=false;button.textContent='수동 결과 적용 누락 점검';}
    });
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-update-redownload]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const task=button.dataset.updateRedownload,draft=manualUpdateRuntime.packets.get(manualPacketKey(room,task));
      if(!draft){notify('진행 중인 작업자료가 없습니다.','warn');return;}
      if(draft.fullBuild)void showManualFullBuildPacket(room,task,{draft,...draft.fullBuild});else openManualUpdateTextDialog(`${MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title} · 작업자료`,`${draft.turnCount}턴 · 결과 기다리는 중 · 작업 ID ${draft.jobId||'이전 작업'}`,draft.packet,'작업자료 복사');
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-update-cancel]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
      const task=button.dataset.updateCancel,job=room.manualPendingJobs?.[task],key=manualPacketKey(room,task);
      if(!job||!confirm(`${MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title} 작업자료 대기를 취소할까요? 저장된 기억과 체크포인트는 유지됩니다.`))return;
      button.disabled=true;
      const prior={...job};
      room.manualPendingJobs[task]={...job,status:'cancelled',draft:null,cancelledAt:nowIso()};
      try{await saveRoom(room);manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(key);renderModalIfOpen();notify('작업자료 대기를 취소했습니다.','success');}
      catch(error){room.manualPendingJobs[task]=prior;button.disabled=false;notify(`작업 취소 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-update-guide]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const task = button.dataset.updateGuide;
      const guide = manualGuideMetadata(room, task);
      openManualUpdateTextDialog(`${MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title} · 지침만 복사`, `${guide.source} · ${guide.version} · ${guide.hash}`, manualGuideOnlyText(room, task), '지침만 복사');
    }));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-update-packet]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => openManualPacketModeDialog(room, button.dataset.updatePacket)));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-update-result]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => openManualUpdateResultDialog(room, button.dataset.updateResult)));
  }

  function loreUiFor(room) {
    const id = String(room?.chatId || '');
    if (!state.loreUi || state.loreUi.chatId !== id) state.loreUi = { chatId:id, search:'', type:'', mode:'', pack:'', openPacks:{}, openCards:{} };
    return state.loreUi;
  }

  function loreModeLabel(mode) {
    return ({ off:'끔', manual:'직접 선택', always:'항상 주입', auto:'감지어 자동' })[mode] || '끔';
  }

  function loreTypeLabel(type) { return type === '물건·장비 설정' ? '아이템' : type || '기타'; }
  function loreCardBadge(card, pack) {
    if (pack.enabled === false || card.enabled === false || card.mode === 'off') return '사용 안 함';
    return ({always:'항상 참고',manual:card.manualSelected === false ? '직접 선택 해제' : '직접 선택',auto:'키워드'})[card.mode] || '사용 안 함';
  }

  function lorePackModeStats(pack) {
    const cards = Array.isArray(pack?.cards) ? pack.cards : [];
    const enabled = cards.filter(card => card?.enabled !== false);
    return {
      total:cards.length,
      auto:enabled.filter(card => card?.mode === 'auto').length,
      always:enabled.filter(card => card?.mode === 'always').length,
      manual:enabled.filter(card => card?.mode === 'manual').length,
      off:cards.filter(card => card?.enabled === false || card?.mode === 'off').length,
    };
  }
  function loreMetaChips(label, values) {
    return Array.isArray(values) && values.length ? `<div class="rpcm-lore-meta-row"><strong>${label}</strong><span>${values.map(value => `<em>${esc(value)}</em>`).join('')}</span></div>` : '';
  }
  function renderLoreCardHtml(pack, card, ui) {
    const fields = loreCardContentFields(card), open = ui.openCards?.[card.id] === true;
    const quote = card.type === '핵심 대사' && fields.exactQuote ? `<blockquote class="rpcm-lore-quote">${esc(fields.exactQuote)}</blockquote><div class="rpcm-lore-quote-meta">${[['화자',fields.speaker],['상대',fields.target],['맥락',fields.context],['장소',fields.location],['날짜',fields.date]].filter(([,value]) => value).map(([label,value]) => `<span><strong>${label}</strong>${esc(value)}</span>`).join('')}</div>` : '';
    const detail = open ? `<div class="rpcm-lore-cardbody">${quote}${fields.full ? `<p class="rpcm-lore-full">${esc(fields.full)}</p>` : ''}${fields.compact && fields.compact !== fields.full ? `<div class="rpcm-lore-density"><strong>짧은 요약</strong><span>${esc(fields.compact)}</span></div>` : ''}${fields.micro && fields.micro !== fields.compact ? `<div class="rpcm-lore-density"><strong>한 줄</strong><span>${esc(fields.micro)}</span></div>` : ''}${loreMetaChips('키워드',fields.triggers)}${loreMetaChips('별칭',fields.aliases)}${loreMetaChips('연결 대상',fields.entities)}<div class="rpcm-lore-card-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-card-edit="${esc(card.id)}">편집</button><details class="rpcm-lore-actions-menu"><summary>⋯ 관리</summary><div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-utility-shortcut="related" data-utility-ref="lore:${esc(card.id)}">관련 기억</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-utility-shortcut="source" data-utility-ref="lore:${esc(card.id)}">근거 찾기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-utility-shortcut="trigger" data-utility-ref="lore:${esc(card.id)}">AI 감지어</button><label>주입 방식<select data-lore-mode="${esc(card.id)}" aria-label="${esc(card.title || '자료 카드')} 주입 방식">${LORE_MODES.map(mode => `<option value="${mode}" ${card.mode === mode ? 'selected' : ''}>${loreModeLabel(mode)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>${card.mode === 'manual' ? `<label class="rpcm-lore-manual-select"><input type="checkbox" data-lore-manual-select="${esc(card.id)}" ${card.manualSelected === false ? '' : 'checked'}> 현재 주입 선택</label>` : ''}<label><input type="checkbox" data-lore-card-enabled="${esc(card.id)}" ${card.enabled === false ? '' : 'checked'}> 카드 사용</label><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn warn" data-lore-card-delete="${esc(card.id)}">삭제</button></div></details></div></div>` : '';
    return `<details class="rpcm-lore-card" data-key="lore-card-${esc(card.id)}" data-lore-card="${esc(card.id)}" ${open ? 'open' : ''}><summary class="rpcm-lore-card-head"><span class="rpcm-lore-card-title"><strong>${esc(card.title || '제목 없음')}</strong><small>${esc(loreTypeLabel(card.type))} · ${loreCardUserLocked(card,pack) ? '사용자 보호' : 'AI 관리'}</small></span><span class="rpcm-lore-mode-badge">${esc(loreCardBadge(card,pack))}</span><span class="rpcm-lore-chevron" aria-hidden="true">⌄</span></summary>${detail}</details>`;
  }

  function renderLoreListHtml(room) {
    const ui = loreUiFor(room), query = ui.search.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
    const packs = lorePacksFor(room).filter(pack => !ui.pack || pack.id === ui.pack);
    if (!packs.length) return '<div class="rpcm-lore-empty">자료팩이 없습니다. 방 전용 진행 자료나 일반 참고 자료팩을 만들어 주세요.</div>';
    const html = packs.map(pack => {
      const cards = (Array.isArray(pack.cards) ? pack.cards : []).filter(card => {
        if (ui.type && card.type !== ui.type) return false;
        if (ui.mode && card.mode !== ui.mode) return false;
        const fields = loreCardContentFields(card);
        return !query || [pack.title,fields.title,fields.full,fields.compact,fields.micro,fields.exactQuote,fields.speaker,fields.target,fields.location,fields.date,...fields.aliases,...fields.triggers,...fields.entities].join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR').includes(query);
      });
      if (!cards.length && (query || ui.type || ui.mode)) return '';
      const open = ui.openPacks?.[pack.id] ?? (pack.kind === 'room' && !pack.copiedFrom);
      const groups = new Map();
      if (open || query) for (const card of cards) { const type = LORE_TYPES.includes(card.type) ? card.type : '기타'; if (!groups.has(type)) groups.set(type,[]); groups.get(type).push(card); }
      const modeStats = lorePackModeStats(pack);
      const body = open || query ? `<div class="rpcm-lore-packbody"><div class="rpcm-lore-pack-actions"><div class="rpcm-lore-pack-state"><label><input type="checkbox" data-lore-pack-enabled="${esc(pack.id)}" ${pack.enabled === false ? '' : 'checked'}> 팩 사용</label><span class="rpcm-lore-pack-mode-summary">자동 ${modeStats.auto}/${modeStats.total} · 항상 ${modeStats.always} · 직접 ${modeStats.manual} · 꺼짐 ${modeStats.off}</span></div><div class="rpcm-lore-pack-tools"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-card-import="${esc(pack.id)}">카드 추가</button><details class="rpcm-update-more rpcm-lore-bulk-menu"><summary>주입 설정</summary><div class="rpcm-update-more-actions"><label>이 팩의 전체 카드<select data-lore-pack-bulk-mode="${esc(pack.id)}" aria-label="${esc(pack.title || '자료팩')} 전체 주입 방식"><option value="">방식 선택…</option><option value="auto">감지어 자동</option><option value="always">항상 주입</option><option value="manual">직접 선택</option><option value="off">사용 안 함</option></select></label><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-pack-bulk-apply="${esc(pack.id)}">선택한 방식 일괄 적용</button></div></details><details class="rpcm-update-more"><summary>팩 관리</summary><div class="rpcm-update-more-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-pack-edit="${esc(pack.id)}">팩 편집</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-card-add="${esc(pack.id)}">직접 작성</button><div class="rpcm-lore-menu-separator" role="separator"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn warn" data-lore-pack-delete="${esc(pack.id)}">팩 삭제</button></div></details></div></div><div class="rpcm-lore-cards">${[...groups].sort(([a],[b]) => LORE_TYPE_ORDER.indexOf(a)-LORE_TYPE_ORDER.indexOf(b)).map(([type,list]) => `<section class="rpcm-lore-type-group" data-key="lore-type-${esc(pack.id)}-${esc(type)}"><div class="rpcm-lore-type-head"><strong>${esc(loreTypeLabel(type))}</strong><small>${list.length}개</small></div>${list.map(card => renderLoreCardHtml(pack,card,ui)).join('')}</section>`).join('') || '<div class="rpcm-lore-empty">표시할 카드가 없습니다.</div>'}</div></div>` : '';
      return `<details class="rpcm-lore-pack" data-key="lore-pack-${esc(pack.id)}" data-lore-pack="${esc(pack.id)}" ${open || query ? 'open' : ''}><summary><span><strong>${esc(pack.title || '제목 없는 자료팩')}</strong><small>${pack.kind === 'room' ? '방 전용 진행 자료' : '일반 참고 자료팩'} · ${cards.length}/${(pack.cards || []).length}카드 · ${lorePackManagedBy(pack) === 'ai' ? 'AI 관리' : '사용자 관리'}</small></span><span class="rpcm-lore-pack-status">${pack.enabled === false ? '사용 안 함' : `사용 중 · 자동 ${modeStats.auto}/${modeStats.total}`}</span><span class="rpcm-lore-chevron" aria-hidden="true">⌄</span></summary>${body}</details>`;
    }).join('');
    return html || '<div class="rpcm-lore-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</div>';
  }

  function renderLoreSectionHtml(room) {
    const ui = loreUiFor(room), packs = lorePacksFor(room);
    const budget = loreBudgetInfoFor(room);
    return `<section class="rpcm-section rpcm-lore-section" id="rpcm-section-lore"><div class="rpcm-section-head"><div><div class="rpcm-section-title">📚 자료집</div><div class="rpcm-section-desc">세계관·장소·조직·규칙 등을 이 방에서 관리하고 RP Manager가 직접 주입합니다. 새 팩은 사용 ON, 새 카드는 감지어 자동입니다. 원치 않으면 팩 사용이나 카드 주입을 끄세요.</div></div><div class="rpcm-charlib-actions rpcm-lore-toolbar"><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn rpcm-add-primary" id="rpcm-lore-add-pack">＋ 자료팩</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" id="rpcm-lore-card-import">카드 추가</button><details class="rpcm-update-more"><summary>가져오기</summary><div class="rpcm-update-more-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" id="rpcm-lore-copy">다른 방에서 선택 복사</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" id="rpcm-lore-direct-paste">JSON 붙여넣기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" id="rpcm-lore-legacy">기존 로어 원문 이전</button></div></details><details class="rpcm-update-more"><summary>관리</summary><div class="rpcm-update-more-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" id="rpcm-lore-json-export">자료집 JSON 내보내기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" id="rpcm-lore-guide">지침</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn" data-utility-shortcut="loreStructure">AI 구조 점검</button></div></details></div></div><div class="rpcm-lore-status">팩 ${packs.length}개 · 카드 ${packs.reduce((n, pack) => n + (pack.cards || []).length, 0)}개 · 주입 상한 ${formatCount(LORE_INJECTION_CHAR_LIMIT)}자/${LORE_INJECTION_CARD_LIMIT}카드 · 핵심 기억 먼저 배치${budget?.candidates ? ` · 후보 ${budget.candidates}개 중 ${budget.included}개 반영${budget.candidates > budget.included ? ' (나머지는 용량 상한으로 제외)' : ''}` : ''} · ${room.pending ? '변경 시 현재 주입 반영 시도' : '다음 주입에 반영'}</div><div class="rpcm-lore-filters"><input id="rpcm-lore-search" type="search" value="${esc(ui.search)}" placeholder="제목·키워드·연결 대상·내용 검색" aria-label="자료집 검색"><select id="rpcm-lore-filter-pack" aria-label="자료팩 필터"><option value="">모든 팩</option>${packs.map(pack => `<option value="${esc(pack.id)}" ${ui.pack === pack.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(pack.title || '제목 없는 팩')}</option>`).join('')}</select><select id="rpcm-lore-filter-type" aria-label="자료 유형 필터"><option value="">모든 유형</option>${LORE_TYPES.map(type => `<option value="${esc(type)}" ${ui.type === type ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(type)}</option>`).join('')}</select><select id="rpcm-lore-filter-mode" aria-label="주입 방식 필터"><option value="">모든 주입 방식</option>${LORE_MODES.map(mode => `<option value="${mode}" ${ui.mode === mode ? 'selected' : ''}>${loreModeLabel(mode)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div id="rpcm-lore-list">${renderLoreListHtml(room)}</div></section>`;
  }

  function lorePortableExport(room) {
    return {
      format:'wish-rp-manager-lore-portable', version:2, exportedAt:nowIso(),
      packs:lorePacksFor(room).map(pack => ({
        id:String(pack.id || ''), title:String(pack.title || ''), kind:String(pack.kind || 'reference'), enabled:pack.enabled !== false,
        managedBy:lorePackManagedBy(pack), copiedFrom:pack.copiedFrom || null, createdAt:pack.createdAt || '', updatedAt:pack.updatedAt || '',
        cards:(pack.cards || []).map(card => ({
          id:String(card.id || ''), ...loreCardContentFields(card), sourceTurnKey:String(card.sourceTurnKey || ''), evidence:String(card.evidence || ''),
          mode:LORE_MODES.includes(card.mode) ? card.mode : 'off', manualSelected:card.manualSelected !== false, enabled:card.enabled !== false,
          managedBy:loreCardManagedBy(card,pack), userLocked:loreCardUserLocked(card,pack), createdAt:card.createdAt || '', updatedAt:card.updatedAt || '',
        })),
      })),
    };
  }

  function openLoreGuideDialog(room) {
    const slot=manualLoreGuideSlot(room);
    const dialog=loreDialog('자료집 지침 · '+(slot.source.includes('사용자')?'사용자 수정본':'기본 v2.0.3'),
      `<p>기본값과 수동 업데이트 패킷은 최종 자료집 v2.0.3 전문을 공유합니다. 이 방의 사용자 수정본은 별도로 유지됩니다.</p><label>지침 전문<textarea name="guide" rows="19" spellcheck="false">${esc(slot.text)}</textarea></label><div class="rpcm-tool-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-guide-copy>내용 복사</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-guide-reset>기본값 복원</button></div>`, async form => {
        const text=form.elements.guide.value;
        if (!text.trim()) throw new Error('빈 지침은 저장할 수 없습니다.');
        const candidate=structuredClone(room);
        candidate.manualUpdateGuides={...(candidate.manualUpdateGuides || {})};
        if (text === MANUAL_LORE_GUIDE_V2) delete candidate.manualUpdateGuides.lore;
        else candidate.manualUpdateGuides.lore=text;
        await saveRoom(candidate);
        rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,candidate);
        manualUpdateRuntime.packets.delete(manualPacketKey(room,'lore'));
        renderModalIfOpen();
      });
    dialog.querySelector('[data-lore-guide-copy]').onclick=async()=>{
      try{if(!await copyPlainText(externalChatTitleInstruction('lore')+'\n\n'+dialog.querySelector('[name="guide"]').value))throw new Error('복사하지 못했습니다.');notify('자료집 지침을 복사했습니다.','success');}
      catch(error){notify(error.message,'error');}
    };
    dialog.querySelector('[data-lore-guide-reset]').onclick=()=>{dialog.querySelector('[name="guide"]').value=MANUAL_LORE_GUIDE_V2;notify('기본 v2.0.3 본문을 불러왔습니다. 저장을 눌러 확정하세요.','success');};
  }

  async function commitLoreMutation(room, mutate) {
    const oldPacks = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(lorePacksFor(room)));
    const oldPending = room.pending ? clonePendingItems(room.pending.items) : null;
    let carrier=room.pending?'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 변경 대상 없음':'로컬 저장 완료 · 다음 주입부터 최신 자료집 사용';
    if (!Array.isArray(room.lorePacks)) room.lorePacks = [];
    try { mutate(); await saveRoom(room); }
    catch (error) { room.lorePacks = oldPacks; throw error; }
    if (room.pending) {
      const signature = items => JSON.stringify((items || []).filter(item => item.group === 'lore').map(item => [item.slotId,item.title,item.content,item.loreMode,item.matchedAlias,item.recallReason]));
      const before = signature(oldPending);
      refreshPendingLoreItems(room);
      try { if (signature(room.pending.items) !== before){const synced=await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'lore-edit');carrier=synced?.suspended||room.pending?.reanchorRetry?'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 대기':'로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 최신 자료집 반영';} }
      catch (error) {
        if (room.pending) { room.pending.items = oldPending; savePendingBackup(room.chatId, room.pending); await saveRoom(room).catch(() => {}); }
        carrier=`로컬 저장 완료 · 현재 주입 반영 실패: ${error.message}`;
        notify(`자료집은 로컬 저장됨 · 현재 주입 반영 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6500);
      }
    }
    const section = state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-section-lore');
    if (section && state.currentRoom === room) {
      rpcmPatchHtml(section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-list'), renderLoreListHtml(room));
      const fresh = document.createElement('template'); fresh.innerHTML = renderLoreSectionHtml(room);
      const nextStatus = fresh.content.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-status');
      const status = section.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-status');
      if (status && nextStatus) status.textContent = nextStatus.textContent;
      for (const id of ['rpcm-lore-search','rpcm-lore-filter-pack','rpcm-lore-filter-type','rpcm-lore-filter-mode']) {
        const live = section.querySelector('#' + id), desired = fresh.content.querySelector('#' + id);
        if (live && desired && document.activeElement !== live) live.value = desired.value;
      }
      state.capacityUiRefresh?.();
      refreshLoreOverviewStatus(state.modal, room);
    } else renderModalIfOpen();
    return {carrier};
  }

  function loreDialog(title, body, onSave) {
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-dialog-backdrop')?.remove();
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
    backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lore-dialog-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-lore-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${esc(title)}"><header><strong>${esc(title)}</strong><button type="button" class="rpcm-lore-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></header><form><div class="rpcm-lore-dialog-body">${body}</div><footer><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-lore-cancel">취소</button><button type="submit" class="rpcm-btn primary">저장</button></footer></form></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    const close = () => backdrop.remove();
    backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-close').onclick = close;
    backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-cancel').onclick = close;
    backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(); };
    backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') close(); };
    backdrop.querySelector('form').onsubmit = async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const button = backdrop.querySelector('[type="submit"]'); button.disabled = true;
      try { await onSave(backdrop.querySelector('form')); close(); notify('자료집 로컬 저장 완료', 'success'); }
      catch (error) { notify(`자료집 저장 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error'); button.disabled = false; }
    };
    backdrop.querySelector('input,textarea,select')?.focus();
    return backdrop;
  }

  function openLorePackEditor(room, pack = null) {
    loreDialog(pack ? '자료팩 편집' : '자료팩 만들기', `<label>자료팩 이름<input name="title" maxlength="120" required value="${esc(pack?.title || '')}" placeholder="예: 현재 방의 진행 자료"></label><label>자료팩 종류<select name="kind"><option value="room" ${pack?.kind === 'room' ? 'selected' : ''}>방 전용 진행형 자료</option><option value="reference" ${pack?.kind !== 'room' ? 'selected' : ''}>일반 참고 자료팩</option></select></label><p>각 방에 독립 저장됩니다. 다른 방 자료는 선택 복사로 가져올 수 있습니다.${pack && lorePackManagedBy(pack) === 'ai' ? ' AI 팩의 이름·종류를 직접 바꾸면 사용자 관리로 전환됩니다.' : ''}</p>`, async form => {
      const title = form.elements.title.value.trim(); if (!title) throw new Error('자료팩 이름을 입력해 주세요.');
      if (!pack) Object.assign(loreUiFor(room), { search:'', type:'', mode:'', pack:'' });
      await commitLoreMutation(room, () => {
        if (pack) { const live = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => item.id === pack.id); if (!live) throw new Error('편집할 자료팩을 찾지 못했습니다.'); if (live.title !== title || live.kind !== form.elements.kind.value) live.managedBy = 'user'; live.title = title; live.kind = form.elements.kind.value; live.updatedAt = nowIso(); }
        else room.lorePacks.push({ id:makeLoreId('pack'), title, kind:form.elements.kind.value, enabled:true, managedBy:'user', cards:[], createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso() });
      });
    });
  }

  async function assertLoreEditedQuote(room, fields) {
    if (fields.type !== '핵심 대사') return;
    if (!fields.exactQuote || !fields.speaker || !fields.context || !fields.sourceTurnKey || !fields.evidence) throw new Error('핵심 대사는 원문 대사·화자·맥락·RP 턴 키·근거가 필요합니다.');
    const turns = await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
    const turn = turns.find(item => String(item.key) === fields.sourceTurnKey);
    if (!turn || !String(turn.text || '').includes(fields.exactQuote)) throw new Error('원문 대사를 지정한 RP 턴에서 연속해서 찾지 못했습니다.');
    const normalize = value => String(value).replace(/\[(?:USER|AI|ASSISTANT)\]/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    if (fields.evidence.length < 2 || fields.evidence.length > 300 || !normalize(turn.text).includes(normalize(fields.evidence))) throw new Error('근거를 지정한 RP 턴에서 확인할 수 없습니다.');
  }

  function openLoreCardEditor(room, pack, card = null, legacyText = '') {
    const split = values => [...new Set(String(values || '').split(/[,，\n]/).map(value => value.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
    const current = loreCardContentFields(card || { content:legacyText });
    const dialog = loreDialog(card ? '자료 카드 편집' : '자료 카드 만들기', `<label>제목<input name="title" maxlength="160" required value="${esc(card?.title || '')}"></label><label>유형<select name="type">${LORE_TYPES.map(type => `<option value="${esc(type)}" ${card?.type === type ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(type)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><div class="rpcm-lore-two"><label>별칭 · 쉼표 또는 줄바꿈<textarea name="aliases" rows="2">${esc(current.aliases.join(', '))}</textarea></label><label>자동 감지어 · 쉼표 또는 줄바꿈<textarea name="triggers" rows="2">${esc(current.triggers.join(', '))}</textarea></label></div><label>연결 대상 · 인물/장소/물건 정본명<textarea name="entities" rows="2">${esc(current.entities.join(', '))}</textarea></label><label>전체 내용 · full<textarea name="full" rows="9">${esc(current.full)}</textarea></label><label>짧은 요약 · compact<textarea name="compact" rows="3">${esc(current.compact)}</textarea></label><label>한 줄 · micro<textarea name="micro" rows="2">${esc(card?.micro ?? current.compact)}</textarea></label><details class="rpcm-lore-editor-source" ${card?.type === '핵심 대사' ? 'open' : ''}><summary>핵심 대사와 원문 근거</summary><div class="rpcm-lore-dialog-body"><label>원문 대사 · exactQuote<textarea name="exactQuote" rows="3">${esc(current.exactQuote)}</textarea></label><div class="rpcm-lore-two"><label>화자<input name="speaker" value="${esc(current.speaker)}"></label><label>상대<input name="target" value="${esc(current.target)}"></label></div><label>장면·상황<input name="context" value="${esc(current.context)}"></label><div class="rpcm-lore-two"><label>장소<input name="location" value="${esc(current.location)}"></label><label>날짜<input name="date" value="${esc(current.date)}"></label></div><label>RP 턴 키<input name="sourceTurnKey" value="${esc(card?.sourceTurnKey || '')}"></label><label>근거 원문<textarea name="evidence" rows="3">${esc(card?.evidence || '')}</textarea></label></div></details><div class="rpcm-lore-two"><label>주입 방식<select name="mode">${LORE_MODES.map(mode => `<option value="${mode}" ${(card?.mode ?? LORE_NEW_CARD_DEFAULTS.mode) === mode ? 'selected' : ''}>${loreModeLabel(mode)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label class="rpcm-lore-check"><input type="checkbox" name="enabled" ${card?.enabled === false ? '' : 'checked'}> 카드 사용</label></div><p>자동 감지는 제목·별칭·감지어로만 합니다. 연결 대상은 검색을 돕습니다. AI 관리 카드의 내용을 직접 바꾸면 이후 AI 갱신에서 보호됩니다.</p>`, async form => {
      const fields = { title:form.elements.title.value.trim(), type:form.elements.type.value, aliases:split(form.elements.aliases.value), triggers:split(form.elements.triggers.value), entities:split(form.elements.entities.value), full:form.elements.full.value.trim(), compact:form.elements.compact.value.trim(), micro:form.elements.micro.value.trim(), exactQuote:form.elements.exactQuote.value.trim(), speaker:form.elements.speaker.value.trim(), target:form.elements.target.value.trim(), context:form.elements.context.value.trim(), location:form.elements.location.value.trim(), date:form.elements.date.value.trim(), sourceTurnKey:form.elements.sourceTurnKey.value.trim(), evidence:form.elements.evidence.value.trim(), mode:form.elements.mode.value, manualSelected:card?.manualSelected !== false, enabled:form.elements.enabled.checked, updatedAt:nowIso() };
      if (!fields.title || !LORE_TYPES.includes(fields.type)) throw new Error('카드 제목과 유형을 확인해 주세요.');
      if (!fields.full && !fields.compact) throw new Error('전체 내용 또는 짧은 요약을 입력해 주세요.');
      for (const name of ['aliases','triggers','entities']) if (fields[name].length > 50 || fields[name].some(value => value.length > 120)) throw new Error(`${name}은 최대 50개, 항목당 120자입니다.`);
      if (fields.type === '핵심 대사') await assertLoreEditedQuote(room,fields);
      fields.content = fields.full; fields.summary = fields.compact;
      if (!fields.micro) fields.micro = fields.compact || fields.full;
      if (!card) Object.assign(loreUiFor(room), { search:'', type:'', mode:'', pack:pack.id });
      await commitLoreMutation(room, () => {
        const livePack = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => item.id === pack.id);
        if (!livePack) throw new Error('대상 자료팩을 찾지 못했습니다.');
        if (card) { const liveCard = (livePack.cards || []).find(item => item.id === card.id); if (!liveCard) throw new Error('편집할 카드를 찾지 못했습니다.'); const contentChanged = JSON.stringify(loreCardContentFields(liveCard)) !== JSON.stringify(loreCardContentFields(fields)) || String(liveCard.sourceTurnKey || '') !== fields.sourceTurnKey || String(liveCard.evidence || '') !== fields.evidence; Object.assign(liveCard, fields); if (contentChanged) liveCard.userLocked = true; }
        else livePack.cards.push({ id:makeLoreId('card'), ...fields, managedBy:'user', userLocked:true, createdAt:nowIso() });
        livePack.updatedAt = nowIso();
      });
    });
    dialog.querySelector('[name="type"]').addEventListener('change', event => {
      if (event.target.value === '핵심 대사') dialog.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-editor-source').open = true;
    });
  }


  function loreImportSplitList(value) {
    return [...new Set(String(value || '').split(/[,，\n]/).map(item => item.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  }

  function loreImportNormalizeCard(raw, label = '자료 카드') {
    const card = raw && typeof raw === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : {};
    const full = String(card.full ?? card.content ?? '').trim();
    const compact = String(card.compact ?? card.summary ?? '').trim();
    const result = {
      title:String(card.title || '').trim(),
      type:String(card.type || '').trim(),
      aliases:Array.isArray(card.aliases) ? card.aliases.map(value => String(value).trim()).filter(Boolean) : loreImportSplitList(card.aliases),
      triggers:Array.isArray(card.triggers) ? card.triggers.map(value => String(value).trim()).filter(Boolean) : loreImportSplitList(card.triggers),
      entities:Array.isArray(card.entities) ? card.entities.map(value => String(value).trim()).filter(Boolean) : loreImportSplitList(card.entities),
      full,
      compact,
      micro:String(card.micro ?? (compact || full)).trim(),
      exactQuote:String(card.exactQuote || '').trim(),
      speaker:String(card.speaker || '').trim(),
      target:String(card.target || '').trim(),
      context:String(card.context || '').trim(),
      location:String(card.location || '').trim(),
      date:String(card.date || '').trim(),
      sourceTurnKey:String(card.sourceTurnKey || '').trim(),
      evidence:String(card.evidence || '').trim(),
    };
    result.aliases = [...new Set(result.aliases)];
    result.triggers = [...new Set(result.triggers)];
    result.entities = [...new Set(result.entities)];
    if (!result.title || result.title.length > 160) throw new Error(`${label}: 제목을 확인해 주세요.`);
    if (!LORE_TYPES.includes(result.type)) throw new Error(`${label}: 허용되지 않은 유형 '${result.type || '(비어 있음)'}'입니다.`);
    if (!result.full && !result.compact) throw new Error(`${label}: 전체 내용 또는 짧은 요약이 필요합니다.`);
    for (const name of ['aliases','triggers','entities']) {
      if (result[name].length > 50 || result[name].some(value => value.length > 120)) throw new Error(`${label}: ${name}은 최대 50개, 항목당 120자입니다.`);
    }
    if (result.type === '핵심 대사' && (!result.exactQuote || !result.speaker || !result.context)) throw new Error(`${label}: 핵심 대사는 exactQuote·speaker·context가 필요합니다.`);
    return result;
  }

  function parseLoreLabelCardText(value, label = 'TXT 카드') {
    const text = String(value || '').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').trim();
    if (!text) throw new Error(`${label}: 빈 파일입니다.`);
    const definitions = [
      ['title', ['제목']],
      ['type', ['유형']],
      ['aliases', ['별칭 · 쉼표 또는 줄바꿈','별칭']],
      ['triggers', ['자동 감지어 · 쉼표 또는 줄바꿈','자동 감지어']],
      ['entities', ['연결 대상 · 인물/장소/물건 정본명','연결 대상']],
      ['full', ['전체 내용 · full','전체 내용']],
      ['compact', ['짧은 요약 · compact','짧은 요약']],
      ['micro', ['한 줄 · micro','한 줄']],
      ['exactQuote', ['핵심 대사 · exactQuote','핵심 대사']],
      ['speaker', ['화자 · speaker','화자']],
      ['target', ['대상 · target','대상']],
      ['context', ['맥락 · context','맥락']],
      ['location', ['장소 · location','장소']],
      ['date', ['날짜 · date','날짜']],
      ['sourceTurnKey', ['원문 턴 키 · sourceTurnKey','원문 턴 키']],
      ['evidence', ['원문 근거 · evidence','원문 근거']],
    ];
    const aliases = new Map();
    for (const [key, labels] of definitions) for (const name of labels) aliases.set(String(name).normalize('NFC'), key);
    const lines = text.split('\n');
    const buckets = {};
    let current = '';
    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
      const rawLine = lines[index];
      const trimmed = rawLine.trim();
      const normalized = trimmed
        .replace(/^#{1,6}\s*/, '')
        .replace(/^\*\*(.*?)\*\*$/, '$1')
        .trim()
        .normalize('NFC');
      if (!current && index === 0 && /^자료\s*카드\s*만들기\s*·/.test(normalized)) continue;
      const key = aliases.get(normalized);
      if (key) {
        const currentValues = buckets[current] || [];
        const hasCurrentValue = currentValues.some(value => String(value || '').trim());
        // title/type은 필수 단일값이다.
        // 특히 type 값 '핵심 대사'는 뒤쪽 exactQuote 라벨과 문자열이 같으므로,
        // 아직 값이 없을 때는 라벨이 아니라 현재 필드 값으로 받아야 한다.
        if (current && ['title','type'].includes(current) && !hasCurrentValue) {
          buckets[current].push(rawLine);
          continue;
        }
        current = key;
        if (!buckets[key]) buckets[key] = [];
        continue;
      }
      if (!current) continue;
      buckets[current].push(rawLine);
    }
    const scalar = key => (buckets[key] || []).join('\n').trim();
    const list = key => loreImportSplitList((buckets[key] || []).join('\n'));
    return loreImportNormalizeCard({
      title:scalar('title'), type:scalar('type'), aliases:list('aliases'), triggers:list('triggers'), entities:list('entities'),
      full:scalar('full'), compact:scalar('compact'), micro:scalar('micro'), exactQuote:scalar('exactQuote'), speaker:scalar('speaker'),
      target:scalar('target'), context:scalar('context'), location:scalar('location'), date:scalar('date'), sourceTurnKey:scalar('sourceTurnKey'), evidence:scalar('evidence'),
    }, label);
  }

  function parseLoreJsonCardText(value, label = 'JSON 카드') {
    const text = String(value || '').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').trim().replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, '').replace(/\s*```$/, '');
    let data;
    try { data = JSON.parse(text); }
    catch (_) { throw new Error(`${label}: JSON 형식을 읽지 못했습니다.`); }
    if (data?.outputs?.lore && typeof data.outputs.lore === 'object') data = data.outputs.lore;
    let cards = [];
    if (data?.version === 'WISH_LORE_V2' && Array.isArray(data.operations)) cards = data.operations.filter(operation => operation?.op === 'card_upsert');
    else if (data?.format === 'wish-rp-manager-lore-portable' && Array.isArray(data.packs)) cards = data.packs.flatMap(pack => Array.isArray(pack?.cards) ? pack.cards : []);
    else if (Array.isArray(data?.cards)) cards = data.cards;
    else if (Array.isArray(data)) cards = data;
    else if (data?.card && typeof data.card === 'object') cards = [data.card];
    else if (data?.title && data?.type) cards = [data];
    if (!cards.length) throw new Error(`${label}: 가져올 자료 카드를 찾지 못했습니다.`);
    return cards.map((card, index) => loreImportNormalizeCard(card, `${label} ${index + 1}`));
  }

  function parseLoreCardFileText(value, label = '카드 파일') {
    const text = String(value || '').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').trim();
    if (!text) throw new Error(`${label}: 빈 파일입니다.`);
    if (/^[\[{]/.test(text) || /^```(?:json)?/i.test(text)) return parseLoreJsonCardText(text, label);
    return [parseLoreLabelCardText(text, label)];
  }

  async function verifyLoreImportedQuote(room, card) {
    if (card.type !== '핵심 대사' || !card.sourceTurnKey || !card.exactQuote) return { checked:false, ok:true };
    const turns = await loadManualCompletedTurns(room);
    const turn = turns.find(item => String(item.key) === card.sourceTurnKey);
    if (!turn) return { checked:true, ok:false, reason:'지정된 RP 턴을 찾지 못함' };
    if (!String(turn.text || '').includes(card.exactQuote)) return { checked:true, ok:false, reason:'지정된 RP 턴에서 exactQuote를 찾지 못함' };
    return { checked:true, ok:true };
  }

  async function readLoreImportFiles(input) {
    const files = [...(input?.files || [])];
    if (!files.length) throw new Error('가져올 TXT 또는 JSON 파일을 선택해 주세요.');
    const cards = [];
    for (const file of files) {
      if (!/\.(?:txt|json)$/i.test(file.name)) throw new Error(`${file.name}: TXT 또는 JSON 파일만 가져올 수 있습니다.`);
      if (file.size > 800000) throw new Error(`${file.name}: 파일이 너무 큽니다.`);
      cards.push(...parseLoreCardFileText(await file.text(), file.name));
    }
    return cards;
  }

  async function openLoreCardImportDialog(room, preferredPackId = '') {
    const packs = lorePacksFor(room);
    if (!packs.length) {
      notify('카드를 넣을 자료팩이 없습니다. 먼저 ‘＋ 자료팩’으로 자료팩을 만들어 주세요.', 'warn', 5200);
      return;
    }
    const selectedPack = packs.find(pack => String(pack.id) === String(preferredPackId || '')) || packs[0];
    const dialog = loreDialog('자료 카드 추가', `<p>ChatGPT·Work 등에서 만든 개별 자료카드 TXT를 그대로 추가할 수 있습니다. 여러 카드를 한 번에 선택해도 됩니다.</p><label>대상 자료팩<select name="pack">${packs.map(pack => `<option value="${esc(pack.id)}" ${selectedPack.id === pack.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(pack.title || '제목 없는 팩')}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>개별 카드 파일<input type="file" name="files" accept=".txt,text/plain,.json,application/json" multiple required></label><label>같은 제목·유형 카드가 이미 있을 때<select name="conflict"><option value="skip">중복 건너뛰기</option><option value="replace">기존 카드 내용 교체 · 기존 주입 설정 유지</option><option value="both">둘 다 보관</option></select></label><div class="rpcm-lore-copy-summary" data-lore-import-summary><p>TXT 형식은 ‘제목 / 유형 / 별칭 / 자동 감지어 / 연결 대상 / full / compact / micro / exactQuote / speaker / target / context / location / date / sourceTurnKey / evidence’ 라벨을 읽습니다.</p></div><p>새 카드는 사용자 보호 카드로 저장되고 ‘감지어 자동’으로 시작합니다. 원치 않으면 카드나 팩 사용을 끌 수 있습니다. sourceTurnKey가 있으면 핵심 대사의 exactQuote를 해당 RP 턴에서 확인합니다.</p>`, async form => {
      const cards = await readLoreImportFiles(form.elements.files);
      const checks = [];
      for (const card of cards) checks.push({ card, result:await verifyLoreImportedQuote(room, card) });
      const failed = checks.filter(item => item.result.checked && !item.result.ok);
      if (failed.length) throw new Error(`${failed[0].card.title}: ${failed[0].result.reason}`);

      const livePacks = lorePacksFor(room);
      const targetPack = livePacks.find(pack => pack.id === form.elements.pack.value) || null;
      if (!targetPack) throw new Error('대상 자료팩을 찾지 못했습니다. 창을 닫고 다시 시도해 주세요.');
      const conflict = form.elements.conflict.value;
      let added = 0, replaced = 0, skipped = 0;
      await commitLoreMutation(room, () => {
        if (!Array.isArray(targetPack.cards)) targetPack.cards = [];
        const keyOf = card => `${String(card.title || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')}\u0000${String(card.type || '')}`;
        for (const imported of cards) {
          const duplicate = targetPack.cards.find(card => keyOf(card) === keyOf(imported));
          if (duplicate && conflict === 'skip') { skipped += 1; continue; }
          const fields = { ...imported, content:imported.full, summary:imported.compact };
          if (duplicate && conflict === 'replace') {
            const preserved = { id:duplicate.id, mode:LORE_MODES.includes(duplicate.mode) ? duplicate.mode : 'off', manualSelected:duplicate.manualSelected !== false, enabled:duplicate.enabled !== false, createdAt:duplicate.createdAt || nowIso() };
            Object.assign(duplicate, fields, preserved, { managedBy:'user', userLocked:true, source:'manual-card-file', updatedAt:nowIso() });
            replaced += 1;
          } else {
            targetPack.cards.push({ id:makeLoreId('card'), ...fields, ...LORE_NEW_CARD_DEFAULTS, managedBy:'user', userLocked:true, source:'manual-card-file', createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso() });
            added += 1;
          }
        }
        targetPack.updatedAt = nowIso();
        const ui = loreUiFor(room);
        Object.assign(ui, { search:'', type:'', mode:'', pack:targetPack.id });
        ui.openPacks[targetPack.id] = true;
      });
      notify(`카드 추가 완료 · 추가 ${added}개 · 교체 ${replaced}개 · 중복 건너뜀 ${skipped}개`, 'success', 6000);
    });
    const submit = dialog.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
    if (submit) submit.textContent = '추가';
    const fileInput = dialog.querySelector('[name="files"]');
    const summary = dialog.querySelector('[data-lore-import-summary]');
    fileInput?.addEventListener('change', async () => {
      try {
        const cards = await readLoreImportFiles(fileInput);
        summary.innerHTML = `<strong>${cards.length}카드 인식</strong><ul>${cards.slice(0, 12).map(card => `<li>${esc(card.type)} · ${esc(card.title)}</li>`).join('')}${cards.length > 12 ? `<li>외 ${cards.length - 12}개</li>` : ''}</ul>`;
      } catch (error) { summary.innerHTML = `<p>파일 확인 실패 · ${esc(error.message)}</p>`; }
    });
  }

  async function openLoreCopyDialog(room) {
    const sources = (await getAllRooms()).filter(other => String(other.chatId) !== String(room.chatId) && lorePacksFor(other).some(pack => (pack.cards || []).length));
    if (!sources.length) { notify('자료집 카드가 있는 다른 방이 없습니다.', 'warn'); return; }
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-backdrop')?.remove();
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
    backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lore-copy-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-lore-dialog rpcm-lore-copy-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="다른 방 자료집 선택 복사"><header><strong>다른 방 자료집 선택 복사</strong><button type="button" class="rpcm-lore-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></header><div class="rpcm-lore-dialog-body"><label>1. 원본 방<select id="rpcm-lore-copy-source"><option value="">원본 방 선택</option>${sources.map(source => `<option value="${esc(source.chatId)}">${esc(source.label || shortId(source.chatId))}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><div id="rpcm-lore-copy-cards"></div><div id="rpcm-lore-copy-preview"></div></div><footer><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-lore-cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" id="rpcm-lore-copy-review" disabled>중복·내용 미리보기</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" id="rpcm-lore-copy-save" disabled>독립 사본 저장</button></footer></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    const close = () => backdrop.remove();
    backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-close').onclick = close;
    backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lore-cancel').onclick = close;
    backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(); };
    backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') close(); };
    const sourceSelect = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-source');
    const cardsHost = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-cards');
    const previewHost = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-preview');
    const reviewButton = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-review');
    const saveButton = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-save');
    let reviewed = null;
    const selected = () => {
      const source = sources.find(item => String(item.chatId) === sourceSelect.value);
      const ids = new Set([...cardsHost.querySelectorAll('[data-lore-copy-card]:checked')].map(node => node.dataset.loreCopyCard));
      const packs = lorePacksFor(source).map(pack => ({ pack, cards:(pack.cards || []).filter(card => ids.has(card.id)) })).filter(item => item.cards.length);
      return { source, packs };
    };
    sourceSelect.onchange = () => {
      reviewed = null; saveButton.disabled = true; previewHost.innerHTML = '';
      const source = sources.find(item => String(item.chatId) === sourceSelect.value);
      cardsHost.innerHTML = source ? `<p>2. 팩과 카드를 고르세요. 상세 내용을 펼쳐 확인할 수 있습니다.</p>${lorePacksFor(source).map(pack => `<details class="rpcm-lore-copy-pack" open><summary>${esc(pack.title || '제목 없는 팩')} · ${(pack.cards || []).length}카드</summary><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-lore-copy-all="${esc(pack.id)}">이 팩 모두 선택</button>${(pack.cards || []).map(card => `<label class="rpcm-lore-copy-row"><input type="checkbox" data-lore-copy-card="${esc(card.id)}"><span><strong>${esc(card.title || '제목 없음')}</strong><small>${esc(card.type || '기타')} · ${esc(loreModeLabel(card.mode))}</small></span></label><details class="rpcm-lore-copy-detail"><summary>내용 확인</summary><pre>${esc(loreCardFullText(card))}</pre></details>`).join('')}</details>`).join('')}` : '';
      reviewButton.disabled = true;
    };
    cardsHost.onchange = () => { reviewed = null; saveButton.disabled = true; previewHost.innerHTML = ''; reviewButton.disabled = !selected().packs.length; };
    cardsHost.onclick = event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('[data-lore-copy-all]'); if (!button) return;
      const { source } = selected(); const pack = lorePacksFor(source).find(item => item.id === button.dataset.loreCopyAll);
      for (const card of pack?.cards || []) { const check = [...cardsHost.querySelectorAll('[data-lore-copy-card]')].find(node => node.dataset.loreCopyCard === card.id); if (check) check.checked = true; }
      cardsHost.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles:true }));
    };
    reviewButton.onclick = () => {
      const choice = selected(); if (!choice.packs.length) return;
      const existing = lorePacksFor(room).flatMap(pack => pack.cards || []);
      const conflicts = choice.packs.flatMap(({ pack, cards }) => cards.map(card => ({ pack, card, duplicate:existing.find(other => String(other.title || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR') === String(card.title || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR') && other.type === card.type) }))).filter(item => item.duplicate);
      reviewed = { sourceId:String(choice.source.chatId), cardIds:choice.packs.flatMap(item => item.cards.map(card => card.id)), conflicts };
      previewHost.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-lore-copy-summary"><strong>3. 중복·충돌 미리보기</strong><p>${choice.packs.length}팩 · ${reviewed.cardIds.length}카드 선택 · 같은 제목/유형 ${conflicts.length}건</p>${conflicts.length ? `<ul>${conflicts.map(item => `<li>${esc(item.pack.title)} / ${esc(item.card.title)} · ${JSON.stringify(loreCardContentFields(item.card)) === JSON.stringify(loreCardContentFields(item.duplicate)) ? '내용 동일' : '내용 차이 있음'}</li>`).join('')}</ul><label>동명 카드 처리<select id="rpcm-lore-copy-conflict"><option value="skip">중복 건너뛰기</option><option value="both">독립 사본으로 함께 보관</option></select></label>` : '<p>동명 카드가 없습니다.</p>'}<p>복사본은 새 ID로 저장하며 팩은 비활성 상태로 시작합니다. 원본과 자동 동기화하지 않습니다.</p></div>`;
      saveButton.disabled = false;
    };
    saveButton.onclick = async () => {
      if (!reviewed) return;
      const choice = selected();
      if (String(choice.source?.chatId || '') !== reviewed.sourceId || choice.packs.flatMap(item => item.cards.map(card => card.id)).join('|') !== reviewed.cardIds.join('|')) { notify('선택이 바뀌었습니다. 미리보기를 다시 확인해 주세요.', 'warn'); saveButton.disabled = true; return; }
      const skip = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy-conflict')?.value !== 'both';
      const duplicateIds = new Set(reviewed.conflicts.map(item => item.card.id));
      const copies = choice.packs.map(({ pack, cards }) => ({ id:makeLoreId('pack'), title:String(pack.title || '복사한 자료팩'), kind:pack.kind === 'room' ? 'room' : 'reference', enabled:false, createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso(), copiedFrom:{ roomId:String(choice.source.chatId), packId:String(pack.id) }, managedBy:'user', cards:cards.filter(card => !skip || !duplicateIds.has(card.id)).map(card => ({ ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(card)), id:makeLoreId('card'), managedBy:'user', userLocked:true, createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso() })) })).filter(pack => pack.cards.length);
      if (!copies.length) { notify('중복을 제외하면 복사할 카드가 없습니다.', 'warn'); return; }
      saveButton.disabled = true;
      try { Object.assign(loreUiFor(room), { search:'', type:'', mode:'', pack:'' }); await commitLoreMutation(room, () => { room.lorePacks = [...lorePacksFor(room), ...copies]; }); close(); notify(`자료집 ${copies.length}팩 · ${copies.reduce((n, pack) => n + pack.cards.length, 0)}카드를 독립 사본으로 저장했습니다.`, 'success'); }
      catch (error) { notify(`자료집 복사 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error'); saveButton.disabled = false; }
    };
    sourceSelect.focus();
  }

  function bindLoreSection(overlay, room) {
    const section = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-section-lore'); if (!section) return;
    bindManualMoreMenus(overlay);
    const redraw = () => { const list = section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-list'); if (list) rpcmPatchHtml(list, renderLoreListHtml(room)); bindManualMoreMenus(overlay); };
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-search').oninput = event => { loreUiFor(room).search = event.target.value; redraw(); };
    for (const [id, field] of [['rpcm-lore-filter-pack','pack'],['rpcm-lore-filter-type','type'],['rpcm-lore-filter-mode','mode']]) section.querySelector('#' + id).onchange = event => { loreUiFor(room)[field] = event.target.value; redraw(); };
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-add-pack').onclick = () => openLorePackEditor(room);
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-card-import').onclick = () => openLoreCardImportDialog(room).catch(error => notify(`카드 추가 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error'));
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-copy').onclick = () => openLoreCopyDialog(room).catch(error => notify(`자료집 복사 화면 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error'));
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-direct-paste').onclick=()=>rpcmDirectPasteDialog('자료집 · JSON 붙여넣기','결과 가져오기가 실패했을 때 쓰는 복구용 증분 반영입니다. WISH_LORE_V2의 현재 P/C REF를 기준으로 적용하며 사용자 보호 카드는 건드리지 않습니다. sourceTurnKey 원문 재검증과 체크포인트 이동은 하지 않습니다.','[[WISH_LORE_V2]]\n{\"version\":\"WISH_LORE_V2\",\"operations\":[...]}\n[[/WISH_LORE_V2]]',()=>JSON.stringify({version:'WISH_LORE_V2',operations:[]},null,2),value=>prepareDirectLorePaste(room,value),prepared=>applyDirectLorePaste(room,prepared));
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-json-export').onclick = () => downloadText(JSON.stringify(lorePortableExport(room),null,2), '자료집_내보내기.json');
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-guide').onclick = () => openLoreGuideDialog(room);
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-legacy').onclick = () => {
      const packs = lorePacksFor(room);
      if (!packs.length) { notify('먼저 자료팩을 하나 만들어 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
      loreDialog('기존 로어 원문 수동 이전', `<p>외부 로어 저장소는 읽거나 삭제하지 않습니다. 기존 로어에서 복사한 원문을 붙여넣고, 대상 팩을 선택하세요. 새 카드는 ‘감지어 자동’으로 시작합니다. 원치 않으면 카드나 팩 사용을 끌 수 있습니다.</p><label>대상 팩<select name="pack">${packs.map(pack => `<option value="${esc(pack.id)}">${esc(pack.title)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>카드 제목<input name="title" required placeholder="원본 자료의 제목"></label><label>원문<textarea name="content" rows="15" required></textarea></label>`, async form => {
        const pack = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => item.id === form.elements.pack.value);
        if (!pack) throw new Error('대상 팩이 없습니다.');
        const title = form.elements.title.value.trim(), content = form.elements.content.value.trim();
        if (!title || !content) throw new Error('제목과 원문을 입력해 주세요.');
        await commitLoreMutation(room, () => pack.cards.push({ id:makeLoreId('card'), type:'기타', title, aliases:[], triggers:[], summary:'', content, ...LORE_NEW_CARD_DEFAULTS, managedBy:'user', userLocked:true, createdAt:nowIso(), updatedAt:nowIso(), source:'manual-legacy-lore' }));
      });
    };
    section.addEventListener('toggle', event => {
      const pack = event.target.closest?.('[data-lore-pack]');
      if (!pack) return;
      const ui = loreUiFor(room);
      if (event.target === pack) {
        const item = lorePacksFor(room).find(value => String(value.id) === pack.dataset.lorePack);
        const expected = !!ui.search.trim() || (ui.openPacks[pack.dataset.lorePack] ?? (item?.kind === 'room' && !item?.copiedFrom));
        if (pack.open === expected) return;
        ui.openPacks[pack.dataset.lorePack] = pack.open; redraw();
      }
      else if (event.target.matches?.('.rpcm-lore-card')) {
        const id = event.target.dataset.loreCard;
        if (event.target.open === (ui.openCards[id] === true)) return;
        ui.openCards[id] = event.target.open; redraw();
      }
    }, true);
    section.addEventListener('click', async event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('button[data-lore-pack-bulk-apply]');
      if (!button) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();

      const packNode = button.closest('[data-lore-pack]');
      const packId = button.dataset.lorePackBulkApply || packNode?.dataset.lorePack;
      const pack = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => String(item.id) === String(packId));
      if (!pack) { notify('자료팩을 찾지 못했습니다.', 'error'); return; }

      const select = packNode?.querySelector(`[data-lore-pack-bulk-mode="${CSS.escape(String(packId))}"]`);
      const mode = String(select?.value || '');
      if (!LORE_MODES.includes(mode)) { notify('적용할 전체 주입 방식을 선택해 주세요.', 'warn'); select?.focus(); return; }

      const count = (pack.cards || []).length;
      if (!count) { notify('이 자료팩에는 카드가 없습니다.', 'warn'); return; }

      const label = loreModeLabel(mode);
      const detail = mode === 'auto'
        ? `카드 ${count}개를 모두 켜고 ‘감지어 자동’으로 바꿉니다. RP에서 제목·별칭·감지어가 관련되면 자동 주입 후보가 됩니다.`
        : mode === 'always'
          ? `카드 ${count}개를 모두 켜고 ‘항상 주입’으로 바꿉니다. 주입 용량이 크게 늘 수 있습니다.`
          : mode === 'manual'
            ? `카드 ${count}개를 모두 켜고 ‘직접 선택’으로 바꿉니다. 기존 직접 선택 상태는 유지합니다.`
            : `카드 ${count}개의 주입 방식을 ‘사용 안 함’으로 바꿉니다. 카드 내용은 삭제하지 않습니다.`;

      if (!confirm(`‘${pack.title || '자료팩'}’ 전체 주입 방식 변경\n\n${detail}\n\n계속할까요?`)) return;

      button.disabled = true;
      try {
        await commitLoreMutation(room, () => {
          const livePack = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => String(item.id) === String(packId));
          if (!livePack) throw new Error('자료팩을 찾지 못했습니다.');
          if (mode !== 'off') livePack.enabled = true;
          for (const card of (livePack.cards || [])) {
            if (!card) continue;
            if (mode === 'off') {
              card.mode = 'off';
            } else {
              card.enabled = true;
              card.mode = mode;
              if (mode === 'manual' && card.manualSelected === undefined) card.manualSelected = true;
            }
            card.updatedAt = nowIso();
          }
          livePack.updatedAt = nowIso();
        });
        notify(`‘${pack.title || '자료팩'}’ ${count}개 카드 · ${label} 일괄 적용 완료`, 'success', 4500);
      } catch (error) {
        notify(`자료팩 일괄 설정 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6500);
      } finally {
        button.disabled = false;
      }
    });

    section.onclick = event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('button[data-lore-pack-edit],button[data-lore-card-add],button[data-lore-card-import],button[data-lore-pack-delete],button[data-lore-card-edit],button[data-lore-card-delete]'); if (!button) return;
      const pack = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => item.id === button.closest('[data-lore-pack]')?.dataset.lorePack);
      const card = (pack?.cards || []).find(item => item.id === button.closest('[data-lore-card]')?.dataset.loreCard);
      if (button.dataset.lorePackEdit) openLorePackEditor(room, pack);
      if (button.dataset.loreCardAdd) openLoreCardEditor(room, pack);
      if (button.dataset.loreCardImport) { void openLoreCardImportDialog(room, pack?.id || button.dataset.loreCardImport).catch(error => notify(`카드 추가 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error')); return; }
